Abstract: Several researchers have reported an atypical inversion of the lowest singlet ($S_{1}$) and triplet ($T_{1}$) excited states in nitrogen-substituted planar hydrocarbons. Contrary to Hund's rule of maximum multiplicity, poly-aza derivatives of phenalenyl, such as cyclazine and heptazine, have long been known to possess a negative singlet-triplet gap, $\Delta E_{ST}=E(S_{1})-E(T_{1})$. Using the well-known Pariser-Parr-Pople (PPP) model, a semi-empirical, wave-function-based Hamiltonian for correlated $\pi$-electrons, we investigate the singlet-triplet gap in ten N-substituted phenalenyl motifs. Two schemes of long-range Coulomb interactions are considered, and key parameters, including the on-site orbital energy, Hubbard U, and hopping integrals, are systematically tuned to benchmark the model for reliably estimating negative singlet-triplet gaps. Full configuration-interaction (FCI) method is employed to incorporate electron-correlation effects. Ground-state and $T_{1}$ absorption spectra are subsequently calculated using the optimized PPP parameters that best reproduce the available experimental data. For validation, first-principles TDDFT calculations using various exchange--correlation functionals are also performed. Conventional adiabatic TDDFT fails to reproduce the inverted ($S_{1}-T_{1}$) ordering, indicating that the inclusion of the correlation driven multiconfigurational effects are essential for describing it. We find that the singlet TDDFT ground-state absorption spectra reproduce several qualitative features of the PPP-FCI spectra, although the TDDFT excitation energies are systematically higher. Detailed analysis of the ground-state and $T_{1}$ absorption spectra reveals significant configuration mixing, providing a comprehensive characterization of the optical response of the N-substituted phenalenyl derivatives studied.

</details>


### [64] [ElectrolyteMD-Bench: How Well Do AI Agents Conduct Molecular Dynamics Studies across Electrolyte Solvation Regimes?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31743)
*Shukai Wu,Shuo Niu,Zhaoming Xu,Yan Luo,Wentao Lin*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 提出 ElectrolyteMD-Bench，评测 AI agents 能否自主完成科学可靠的锂电池电解液分子动力学研究；结果显示虽能跑通流程，但无一研究满足全部科学要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI agents 正推动分子动力学研究自动化，有望加速锂电池电解液发现与设计，但其能否自主完成科学可靠的电解液 MD 研究尚缺乏充分评估。

Method: 构建覆盖稀溶液、高浓度和局部高浓度电解质的基准，将局部配位、离子缔合、单粒子动力学与集体输运纳入统一研究流程；agents 在冻结科学模型下自主完成体系构建、平衡、生产采样、性质分析与解释，并决定纠错、继续采样或停止；从模拟有效性、分析有效性、证据完整性和结果校准四个维度审计。

Result: 在 8 个模型-工具配置、24 个体系运行中，所有运行均产生有效生产轨迹，但 8 项研究无一满足全部科学要求；失败包括缺失分析、物理定义或数值实现错误、统计支持不足，以及完成声明与保留证据不一致。

Conclusion: 结果暴露了成功执行 MD 工作流与可靠完成科学研究之间的差距；ElectrolyteMD-Bench 提供了基础测试，并定义了自主电解液模拟迈向计算优先材料探索所需跨越的关键能力阈值。

Abstract: AI agents are advancing the automation of molecular dynamics (MD) research, with the potential to accelerate discovery and design of lithium-battery electrolytes. However, whether they can autonomously complete scientifically reliable electrolyte MD studies remains insufficiently evaluated. We introduce ElectrolyteMD-Bench, which unifies dilute, high-concentration, and localized high-concentration electrolytes in a comparative study across distinct solvation regimes. The benchmark connects local coordination, ionic association, single-particle dynamics, and collective transport within one continuous research process. Agents autonomously perform system construction, equilibration, production sampling, property analysis, and interpretation under a frozen scientific model, while deciding whether to correct errors, continue sampling, or stop. We audit each study along four dimensions: simulation validity, analysis validity, evidence integrity, and outcome calibration. Across eight model--harness configurations, all 24 system runs produced valid production trajectories, yet none of the eight studies satisfied all scientific requirements. Failures included missing analyses, incorrect physical definitions or numerical implementations, insufficient statistical support, and completion claims inconsistent with retained evidence. These results expose a gap between successful MD workflow execution and reliable scientific study completion. ElectrolyteMD-Bench therefore provides a foundational test and defines a key capability threshold that autonomous electrolyte simulation must cross to advance toward computation-first materials exploration.

</details>


### [65] [Accelerating Quantum Simulations of Materials Through Parameter and Ansatz Transfer Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33617)
*Saurabh Shivpuje,Vinit Singh,Manas Sajjan,Sabre Kais*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出在相关哈密顿量族之间迁移变分信息（参数与拟设）的策略，以加速凝聚态电子结构的量子模拟，并在锂电池正极材料 Li$_x$CoO$_2$ 上验证了效率与精度优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算有望用于电子结构计算，但实际凝聚态问题常需处理由 k 点采样、组分变化、缺陷和系统尺寸变化产生的大量相关哈密顿量族，直接逐个求解代价高。

Method: 针对相同嵌入尺寸的哈密顿量，迁移已优化的变分参数；针对不同尺寸，提出 AARTI（Augmented Ansatz Reuse for Target Initialization），迁移兼容的 Pauli-generator 结构与源优化参数，并用目标特定生成器扩展拟设。流程结合 DFT、Wannier 下折叠、嵌入紧束缚哈密顿量、变分量子算法和神经网络量子态，并在 NVIDIA CUDA-Q 平台进行量子电路模拟。

Result: 以 Li$_x$CoO$_2$ 为代表性电池正极材料，研究涵盖多个脱锂水平、密集 k 点网格和递增尺寸的哈密顿量族。参数与拟设迁移显著减少了求解相关哈密顿量所需的优化步数，同时保持共同目标精度，部分情况下终端误差更低。计算得到的脱锂电子结构演化表现出明显的非刚性带行为，与实验一致。

Conclusion: 结果建立了利用相关哈密顿量之间相似性来提升真实凝聚态系统量子工作流效率与可扩展性的框架。

Abstract: Quantum computing offers a promising route for electronic-structure calculations, but practical condensed-matter applications often require solving large families of related Hamiltonians arising from $\mathbf{k}$-point sampling, compositional variation, defects, and changes in system size. Here, we develop strategies for transferring variational information between related Hamiltonians to accelerate quantum simulations. For Hamiltonians of the same embedded size, optimized variational parameters are transferred between related calculations. For different sizes, we introduce Augmented Ansatz Reuse for Target Initialization (AARTI), which transfers compatible Pauli-generator structure and optimized source parameters, then augments the ansatz with target-specific generators. Our workflow combines density functional theory, Wannier downfolding, embedded tight-binding Hamiltonians, variational quantum algorithms, and neural-network quantum states, with quantum-circuit simulations implemented using NVIDIA's CUDA-Q platform. Using Li$_x$CoO$_2$ as a representative battery cathode material, we study Hamiltonian families spanning multiple delithiation levels, dense $\mathbf{k}$-point meshes, and increasing sizes. Parameter and ansatz transfer substantially reduce the optimization steps required to solve these related Hamiltonians while maintaining the common target accuracy, with lower terminal errors in several cases. The calculated electronic-structure evolution with delithiation exhibits pronounced non-rigid-band behavior consistent with experiment. These results establish a framework for exploiting similarity among related Hamiltonians to improve the efficiency and scalability of quantum workflows for realistic condensed-matter systems.

</details>


### [66] [A Green-function unification of weak interactions with environmental dressing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31728)
*Johannes Fiedler*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 提出一个统一的宏观量子电动力学框架，将中性或带电体系间的弱非共价相互作用统一由单一相互作用哈密顿量和电磁格林函数描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静电、诱导和色散等弱相互作用通常被视为不同物理机制，并采用各自独立的模型与近似处理，缺乏统一描述。

Method: 从任意线性环境中的宏观QED出发，采用一致的多极展开处理单极、偶极和四极，推导出可生成永久静电、混合诱导和完全诱导（色散）相互作用的紧凑主公式；所有相互作用通道均由多极矩、响应函数与格林张量的缩并得到，环境影响通过 dressed Green functions 统一引入。

Result: 在非延迟自由空间极限下，形式体系统一恢复 Keesom、Debye 和 London/van der Waals 相互作用；一般表达式还可通过直接代入界面、限域或可极化连续介质等对应格林函数，处理不同物理场景而无需重新构造相互作用。

Conclusion: 该框架将格林函数提升为复杂环境中弱相互作用的普适组织原则，实现了不同弱相互作用机制和环境效应的统一描述。

Abstract: Weak, non-covalent interactions such as electrostatics, induction, and dispersion are commonly treated as distinct physical mechanisms, often using separate models and approximations. Here, we present a unified quantum-electrodynamical framework in which all weak interactions between neutral or charged subsystems emerge from a single interaction Hamiltonian and are expressed entirely in terms of electromagnetic Green functions.
  Starting from macroscopic QED in arbitrary linear environments, we derive compact master formulas that generate permanent electrostatic, mixed induction, and fully induced (dispersion) interactions on equal footing. Monopoles, dipoles, and quadrupoles are treated consistently within the same multipole expansion, leading to a common structural form in which all interaction channels are obtained from contractions of multipole moments and response functions with Green tensors. Environmental effects enter universally through dressed Green functions.
  The standard non-retarded free-space limits, including Keesom, Debye, and London/van der Waals interactions, are recovered as a consistency check of the formalism. Beyond this validation, the formulation shows that once the general expressions are established, different physical scenarios, such as interfaces, confinement, or polarisable continua, can be treated by direct substitution of the corresponding Green function, without reformulating the interaction for each case. This elevates Green functions to a universal organising principle for weak interactions in complex environments.

</details>


### [67] [Pressure and temperature dependence of methane pyrolysis and hydrogen production in a micro flow reactor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31741)
*Mohammad R. Razavi,Ömer L. Gülder*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 在微型流动反应器中开展甲烷热解实验，考察高压高温下化学机理敏感性，并与1D离散截面模型模拟对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为甲烷制氢构建可独立控制反应参数的实验平台，并评估现有动力学/颗粒与表面沉积模型在高温高压甲烷热解中的预测能力。

Method: 微型流动反应器，温度1400–1800 K、压力1–10 bar、停留时间60 ms；定量H2、未反应CH4、固体碳及非均相沉积质量；GC-MS检测C2和PAH；与1D离散截面模型模拟对比。

Result: 模拟在1800 K下能较好复现压力趋势，但温度敏感性不符；模拟普遍给出更低碳/氢产率和更强温度敏感性。低温高压下测得大量PAH中间体；模拟表面沉积速率低于实验值但剖面相似。

Conclusion: 微型流动反应器可用于再现甲烷热解制氢环境；需改进低温燃料分解及PAH生成/消耗动力学，并细化表面位点密度处理以提升模拟预测能力。

Abstract: Methane pyrolysis experiments were performed in a micro flow reactor to investigate the chemical mechanism sensitivities at elevated pressures and temperatures. The micro flow reactor used in this study provides independent control over reaction parameters. Experiments were performed at temperatures from 1400 to 1800 K and pressures from 1 to 10 bar at a fixed residence time of 60 ms. The yields of hydrogen, unreacted methane, and solid carbon were quantified for comparison to predictive models. The mass of heterogeneous depositions was also quantified. The presence of C2 and PAH species were measured by GC-MS. The results were compared to 1D simulations using a discrete sectional model for capturing the kinetics of particulate formation and surface deposition. The simulations replicated the pressure trend well at high temperatures (1800 K), but the sensitivity to temperature was in disagreement. Simulations generally found lower carbon and hydrogen yields and a stronger sensitivity to temperature than the experiment. An abundance of PAH intermediates was measured at lower temperatures and higher pressures. The simulated surface deposition rates were lower than the measured values but showed similar profiles. The results demonstrate the utility of micro flow reactors in recreating pyrolysis environments for hydrogen production from methane. The predictive capabilities of simulations could be improved by advancing the fuel decomposition and PAH formation and consumption kinetics at low temperatures and by detailed treatment of surface site density.

</details>


### [68] [Heteroatom Position Controls Ultrafast Photodynamics of Oxazole and Isoxazole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31875)
*Briony Downes-Ward,Paul Javed,Huynh V. S. Lam,Sajib K. Saha,Surjendu Bhattacharyya,Martin Centurion,Xinxin Cheng,R. Joel England,Casey Foley,Smita Ganguly,Patrick L. Kramer,Jinxin Lang,Randy Lemons,Ming-Fu Lin,Yusong Liu,Tu T. Nguyen,Joao Pedro Figueira Nunes,Chatura Perera,Alexander H. Reid,Ethan Ross,Artem Rudenko,Xiaozhe Shen,John Searles,Anbu Selvam Venkatachalam,Enliang Wang,Stephen P. Weathersby,Vinod Kumarappan,Arthur G. Suits,Christine Aikens,Daniel Rolles*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该工作利用MeV超快电子衍射结合轨迹面跳跃模拟，首次实时表征了噁唑与异噁唑的光诱导开环及后续碎片化，揭示杂原子位置如何决定超快反应路径与产物分支。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 杂环化合物的超快光化学在光生物学和材料化学中至关重要，但其结构动力学的直接实验观测仍很稀少。

Method: 在200 nm光激发下，采用MeV超快电子衍射（UED）对噁唑和异噁唑异构体进行实时结构表征，并结合非绝热分子动力学与轨迹面跳跃（TSH）模拟；将轨迹系综模拟衍射图与实验仪器响应函数卷积后，与UED分支比比较。

Result: 两种异构体均发生开环并碎片化，但动力学显著不同。异噁唑所有轨迹在40 fs内专一发生N-O键断裂，随后在数百飞秒尺度依次形成HCN+乙烯酮和HCO+乙烯基氮宾通道。噁唑开环较慢（290 fs）且效率仅85%，主要经O-C键断裂，并出现腈叶立德、O-锥形化结构等更丰富的中间体。模拟衍射信号与实验分支比一致。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了原子尺度图像，表明结构异构体中杂原子连接方式的简单交换会从根本上重塑激发态势能面、圆锥交叉可达性及光化学结果。

Abstract: The ultrafast photochemistry of heterocyclic compounds is central to photobiology and materials chemistry, yet direct experimental observation of their structural dynamics remains rare. Here we present the first real-time structural characterization of photoinduced ring opening and subsequent fragmentation in the isomeric pair oxazole and isoxazole using MeV ultrafast electron diffraction (UED), complemented by non-adiabatic molecular dynamics simulations. Upon photoexcitation at 200 nm, both isomers undergo ring opening followed by fragmentation into various products, but with strikingly different dynamics governed by heteroatom positioning. Trajectory Surface Hopping simulations reproduce the experimental diffraction signatures, which are surprisingly similar for both isomers, and reveal distinct mechanistic pathways for the two isomers. For isoxazole, all trajectories exclusively undergo N-O bond cleavage within 40 fs, followed by sequential fragmentation into HCN + ketene and HCO + vinyl nitrene channels on the hundreds-of-femtoseconds timescale. Oxazole, by contrast, shows significantly slower ring opening (290 fs) with only 85% efficiency, proceeding primarily through O-C cleavage and accessing a richer landscape of intermediates including nitrile ylide and O-pyramidalized structures. Simulated diffraction patterns derived from trajectory ensembles, convolved with the experimental instrument response function, are in agreement with the branching ratios observed by UED. This synergy between UED and trajectory surface hopping provides an atomistic picture of how the simple interchange of heteroatom connectivity in structural isomers fundamentally reshapes excited-state potential surfaces, conical intersection accessibility, and photochemical outcome.

</details>


### [69] [Bridging Molecular Scales with Implicit Score Matching for Bottom-Up Coarse Graining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32040)
*Patrick G. Sahrmann,Nicholas Lubbers,Benjamin T. Nebgen,Emily Shinkle,Sergei Tretiak,Kipton Barros,Brenden W. Hamilton*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于隐式分数匹配的粗粒化分子动力学框架，无需显式粗粒化力，可构建高保真、极高粗粒化程度的机器学习模型，桥接分子与介观尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子动力学受限于时空尺度，粗粒化可扩展尺度，但构建准确且高度粗粒化的势函数困难，因为粗粒化力依赖复合原子构型的统计分布，直接计算代价过高。

Method: 引入隐式分数匹配框架，直接避开显式粗粒化力的计算；采用自下而上的机器学习建模，显著减少训练数据需求，并允许每个粗粒化位点代表数百至数千个原子。

Result: 在多种化学体系和现象中验证了方法的通用性与有效性；模型可高效模拟微米至毫米尺度，同时保留大量原子尺度保真度。

Conclusion: 隐式分数匹配是实现化学准确且连接分子与介观尺度模拟的实用途径。

Abstract: Molecular dynamics simulations provide a computational microscope for atomic scale processes but remain restricted to relatively small spatial and temporal scales. Coarse-grained models extend their reach by representing groups of atoms as effective interaction sites. The number of particles is systematically reduced by embedding the collective behavior of groups of atoms into coarse-grained sites governed by effective potentials. However, constructing accurate, highly coarsened potentials, even with the assistance of modern machine-learning methods, is a grand challenge because coarse grained forces depend on a statistical distribution of composite atomic configurations making direct calculation computationally prohibitive. Here, we introduce an alternative framework based on implicit score matching that directly avoids the need for explicit coarse-grained forces. This approach both substantially reduces the training data required relative to conventional force-matching methods and enables high-fidelity models in which each coarse-grained site represents hundreds to thousands of atoms. We demonstrate the method's versatility and effectiveness across a diverse range of chemical systems and phenomena, developing bottom-up, machine-learned models. These enable efficient modeling at micrometer and millimeter length scales, while retaining significant amounts of the atomic scale fidelity. These results establish implicit score matching as a practical route towards chemically accurate simulations that bridge molecular and mesoscopic scales.

</details>


### [70] [Reinforcement learning amortizes transition-state physics into one-step flow models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32073)
*Yunyang Li,Zechang Sun,Kuang Yu,Wen Yan,Mark Gerstein,Hung Q. Pham*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: Trèfle 是一种一步流生成模型，将物理约束通过均值流目标、低成本代理模型和强化学习后训练内化到训练阶段，从而快速生成过渡态候选，避免昂贵的多步采样与推理时引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过渡态搜索是反应发现的主要瓶颈，需要大量昂贵量子化学计算；现有生成模型虽可从反应物和产物几何提出候选，但仅用几何监督训练无法满足过渡态所需物理条件，而在采样时施加约束又代价高昂。

Method: Trèfle 采用一步流模型，将物理引导生成摊销到训练中：用均值流目标替代多步采样，用低成本代理模型替代 DFT 奖励评估，并用强化学习后训练与 enforcing 物理条件的奖励替代推理时引导。

Result: Trèfle 生成过渡态的吞吐量是多步基线的 30 倍；可少样本迁移到 10 种未见过渡金属，并零样本泛化到远大于训练集的分子。结合 DFT 精修与反应路径验证，Trèfle 种子搜索恢复了全部 13 个未见 γ-酮基过氧化氢通道；后训练将 85 个未见双分子通道的单次尝试恢复率从 28% 提升至 50%。

Conclusion: Trèfle 为自动化反应发现提供了快速、可靠的过渡态种子。

Abstract: Transition-state searches remain a major bottleneck in reaction discovery, as identifying valid saddle-point structures requires numerous expensive quantum-chemical calculations. Generative models can reduce this burden by proposing candidates from reactant and product geometries, but supervised training on geometries alone does not enforce the physical conditions required of a transition state, while enforcing them during sampling is costly. We introduce Tr\`efle, a one-step flow model that amortizes physics-guided generation into training: a mean-flow objective replaces multi-step sampling, a low-cost surrogate replaces density functional theory (DFT) reward evaluations, and reinforcement-learning post-training with rewards that enforce those conditions replaces inference-time guidance. Tr\`efle generates accurate transition states at $30\times$ the throughput of multi-step baselines. It transfers few-shot to ten unseen transition metals and generalizes zero-shot to molecules far larger than any in training. With DFT refinement and reaction-path validation, Tr\`efle-seeded searches recover all 13 unseen $\gamma$-ketohydroperoxide channels, while post-training raises per-attempt recovery from 28% to 50% across 85 unseen bimolecular channels. Tr\`efle thus provides fast, reliable seeds for automated reaction discovery.

</details>


### [71] [The Same and Not the Same: A Machine-Checked Genealogy of Photochemical Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32251)
*Junyi Gong,Zijie Qiu,Ben Zhong Tang*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: PhotoLean 用 Lean 4 形式化了十七个光化学与光物理理论，并在共享内核上机器验证全部 136 对理论间关系，同时裁决三处继承性概念混淆，提出理论本身的可审计关系账本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学现象学理论随可观测量增殖：同一物理实在在不同测量语境中被反复命名，而理论间关系一直缺乏可审计的载体。

Method: 在 Lean 4 共享内核上形式化十七个光化学与光物理理论，构建机器检验的谱系；对每一对理论记录已验证关系（等价、蕴含、带注册前提的复合、定义性证书）或缺失关系的注册理由，覆盖全部 136 对；并用当且仅当边界与见证裁决三处继承混淆。

Result: 全部 136 对理论关系均被覆盖。三处裁决为：对称因子 β 等于 1/2 当且仅当反应物与产物力常数相等；Kasha 规则与 Kasha-Vavilov 规则仅在带损失通道的封闭量化下一致；仅强度 Stern-Volmer 数据不能区分静态与动态猝灭，而寿命通道可精确区分。

Conclusion: 当命题生成转向机器时，此类机器检验的关系账本为理论本身提供了可复制的审计层。

Abstract: Chemistry's phenomenological theories multiply with its observables: the same physical reality is repeatedly named in different measurement contexts, and the relations among theories have never had an auditable carrier. Here we present PhotoLean, a machine-checked genealogy of seventeen photochemical and photophysical theories formalized in Lean 4 over a shared kernel. Every pair of theories carries either a verified relation (equivalence, implication, composition with registered premises, definitional certificate) or a registered reason for its absence, covering all 136 pairs. Three adjudications resolve inherited conflations with if-and-only-if boundaries and witnesses: the symmetry factor $\beta$ equals one-half exactly when reactant and product force constants coincide; Kasha's rule and the Kasha-Vavilov rule agree only under closed quantification with a loss channel; and intensity-only Stern-Volmer data cannot distinguish static from dynamic quenching, whereas the lifetime channel discriminates exactly. As proposition generation shifts to machines, such machine-checked relational ledgers offer a replicable audit layer for theory itself.

</details>


### [72] [Reduced scaling implementation of the coupled cluster-based static embedding method, MPCC with density fitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32884)
*Avijit Shee,Fabian M. Faulstich,K. Birgitta Whaley,Lin Lin,Martin Head-Gordon*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于密度拟合的改进 MPCC 静态量子嵌入算法，可显著降低计算成本并保持与原始实现相近的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始 MPCC 方法精度较好但计算成本高，需要低标度实现以扩展到更大体系。

Method: 在所有嵌入层（低级别方法、屏蔽相互作用、高级别方法）对电子排斥积分采用 DF 近似；低级别方法使用基于 Laplace 变换的 Sylvester 方程近似因子分解解；屏蔽相互作用采用基于 DCA 的近似构造，忽略部分高阶交换与 ladder 图。复杂度为低级别 O(N^4)、屏蔽相互作用非迭代 N_frag O(N^4)、片段求解迭代 O(N_frag^6)。

Result: 应用于 trans 聚乙炔 C2nH2n+2（n=1-8）并与原始未因子化实现比较时间和精度，还验证了 O3 势能面以及 MP2 表现较差的 S22 子集分子。

Conclusion: DF 版 MPCC 在保持与原始实现相当精度的同时显著降低计算成本。

Abstract: An improved algorithm to implement our previously proposed static quantum embedding method MPCC (J. Chem. Phys. 161, 164107 (2024)) is presented. It uses the density-fitting (DF) approximation for the electron-repulsion integrals (ERIs) across all layers of the embedding method: the low-level method, the screened interaction, and the high-level method. Additionally, an approximate factorized solution of the Sylvester equation is employed based on the Laplace transformation for the low-level method. To build the screened interaction, we have explored an approximate construction of it based on the distinguishable cluster approximation (DCA) by Kats and Manby (J. Chem. Phys. 141, 061101 (2014)), which amounts to neglecting certain higher-order exchange and ladder diagrams. Combining all these techniques, we have obtained an $\mathcal{O}(N^4)$ algorithm for the low-level method; a non-iterative $N_{\rm frag} \mathcal{O}(N^4)$ algorithm for the screened interaction; and an iterative $\mathcal{O}(N_{\rm frag}^6)$ algorithm for the fragment solver, where $N_{\rm frag}$ is the number of fragment orbitals and $N$ is the total number of orbitals. The resulting low-scaling implementation of the MPCC method is applied to trans polyacetylenes, that is, C$_{2n}$H$_{2n+2}$ molecules for chain lengths $n=1-8$, and the timing and accuracy of the method are compared to the original unfactorized implementation. Moreover, the approximations are further validated by applying the method to the potential energy surface of the \ce{O3} molecule. Finally, we apply the method to the selected molecules in the S22 dataset for which the performance of the MP2 method is known to be poor. The results demonstrate that the DF-based implementation of the MPCC method provides a significant reduction in computational cost while maintaining accuracy comparable to that of the original implementation.

</details>


### [73] [Machine-learned Laplacian-level density functional from exact exchange-correlation potentials and energies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34194)
*Arghadwip Paul,Bikash Kanungo,Sambit Das,Vikram Gavini*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 提出 NNLap：一种机器学习得到的 Laplacian 级交换-相关泛函，在 PBE 基础上加入依赖电子密度、梯度与 Laplacian 的神经网络修正，仅用少量体系训练即可达到 meta-GGA 精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索能否用 Laplacian 级模型、基于精确 XC 势与能量训练，在不依赖轨道的情况下达到 meta-GGA 泛函的精度。

Method: 以 PBE 为基线，构建输入为电子密度及其梯度、Laplacian 的神经网络修正；训练数据来自对 CI 密度进行逆 DFT 得到的精确 XC 势与能量，仅涉及 5 个原子和 3 个分子。

Result: 在热化学基准上可与 SCAN、r2SCAN 竞争；总能量精度与 SCAN 相当，优于 r2SCAN 和 B3LYP。

Conclusion: Laplacian 级机器学习泛函，若以精确 XC 势与能量训练，可在无轨道依赖条件下达到 meta-GGA 的精度。

Abstract: We present NNLap, a machine-learned Laplacian-level exchange-correlation (XC) functional that augments PBE with a neural-network correction depending on the electron density, its gradient, and its Laplacian. The model is trained on exact XC potentials and energies, obtained through inverse density-functional theory (DFT) calculations on configuration-interaction densities. Despite training on only a few systems -- five atoms and three molecules -- the model achieves remarkable accuracy on thermochemistry benchmarks, competing with the meta-GGA functionals SCAN and r2SCAN. It also attains accurate total energies, comparable to SCAN and better than r2SCAN and B3LYP. This shows that a Laplacian-level model, trained on exact XC potentials and energies, can reach the accuracy of meta-GGAs without their orbital dependence.

</details>


### [74] [Curvature-Aware Flow Matching for Molecular Structure Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34592)
*Samir Darouich,Juliane Geisler,Jacob W. Toney,Tanja Bien,Johannes Kästner,Heather J. Kulik,Mathias Niepert*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: CurvFM 在流匹配训练中用冻结的 Hessian 预测模型约束生成分子结构的局部曲率，从而在保持全局结构精度的同时显著提升物理保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型虽能高精度生成三维分子结构，但训练时未显式利用物理信息。势能面（PES）决定分子几何附近的能量变化，而 RMSD 仅间接反映局部势能面；构象和过渡态（TS）是力为零的驻点，一阶信息不足，关键在 Hessian 曲率。量子化学 Hessian 训练成本过高，近期机器学习 Hessian 预测器使曲率监督变得可行。

Method: 提出 CurvFM，一种流匹配（FM）公式，其训练目标约束生成结构处的曲率。该方法使用冻结的 Hessian 预测模型，在生成端点和参考结构处评估，从而无需量子化学 Hessian，并使残差足够廉价以在每次训练步骤中施加。

Result: 在过渡态和构象生成中，CurvFM 在全局结构精度不变的情况下显著提升物理保真度：在 GEOM-QM9 上将最大力中位偏差降低 63%，并在三个反应数据集上将有效 TS 比例最多提高 10%。

Conclusion: 通过流匹配直接监督机器学习预测的 Hessian 曲率，可低成本地向分子生成模型注入物理真实性，同时不牺牲全局结构精度。

Abstract: Generative models produce three-dimensional molecular structures with high geometric accuracy, yet they are trained on distributions of atomic coordinates without explicit access to the underlying physics. The potential energy surface (PES) describes how the energy changes around a molecular geometry, and the RMSD between a generated structure and its reference captures this local landscape only indirectly. Supervising the PES during training could close this gap, but conformers and transition states (TSs) are stationary points at which the forces vanish, so first-order quantities carry little information about them. What remains is the local curvature, encoded in the Hessian of the PES. Quantum-chemical Hessians are too expensive to be available at the scale required for training, but recently proposed machine-learned predictors make curvature inexpensive enough to supervise directly. We introduce CurvFM, a flow matching (FM) formulation whose training objective constrains the curvature at the generated structure. A frozen Hessian prediction model is evaluated at the generated endpoint and at the reference structure, which removes the need for quantum-chemical Hessians and keeps the residual cheap enough to impose at every training step. Across TS and conformer generation, CurvFM substantially improves physical fidelity at unchanged global structural accuracy, reducing the median deviation in maximum force by 63% on GEOM-QM9 and raising the fraction of valid TSs by up to 10% across three reaction datasets.

</details>


### [75] [Symmetry-Aware Flow Matching for End-to-end Molecular Crystal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34690)
*Wendi Cai,Fanyang Mo*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: SALA 是一个基于 flow matching 的分子晶体生成模型，通过状态-上下文分离，仅演化不对称单元（ASU）坐标和对称兼容晶格参数，同时从完整周期环境预测更新；在给定分子图和空间群条件下，端到端生成分子构象、晶格几何与堆积，并保持指定对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子晶体堆积决定药物生物利用度、电荷传输等性质，但生成真实结构需同时协调分子柔性、分子间相互作用和对称性。现有原子级全胞生成方法命中率低、堆积 RMSD 高。

Method: SALA 采用状态-上下文分离的 flow-matching 框架：状态仅包含不对称单元原子坐标与对称兼容晶格参数，上下文为完整周期环境；模型以分子图和空间群为条件，在保持指定对称性的同时，端到端生成分子构象、晶格几何和晶体堆积。

Result: 在涵盖四个化学类别和全部七个晶系的 799 个留出晶体上，SALA 每目标 50 个候选达到 72.0% 命中率，而原子级全胞生成基线为 8.1%；平均最佳候选堆积 RMSD 从 4.48 Å 降至 1.70 Å，且随着体系尺寸和对称多重性增加，优势进一步扩大。

Conclusion: SALA 能端到端生成复杂且受对称性约束的分子晶体，在晶体结构预测（CSP）、基于结构的药物多晶型设计以及有机分子材料发现中具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Molecular crystal packing shapes properties from drug bioavailability to charge transport, yet generating realistic structures requires coordinating molecular flexibility, intermolecular interactions and symmetry. Here we introduce SALA, a flow-matching model built on state--context separation: it evolves only asymmetric-unit (ASU) atomic coordinates and symmetry-compatible lattice parameters while predicting their updates from the full periodic environment. Conditioned on molecular graphs and a space group, SALA generates molecular conformation, lattice geometry and packing end to end while preserving the specified symmetry. Across 799 held-out crystals spanning four chemical classes and all seven crystal systems, SALA achieves a 72.0\% hit rate from 50 candidates per target, compared with 8.1\% for an atomistic full-cell generative baseline. Mean best-candidate packing root-mean-square deviation decreases from 4.48 to 1.70~\AA, with the advantage widening as system size and symmetry multiplicity increase. SALA thus enables end-to-end generation of complex, symmetry-constrained molecular crystals, with potential applications in CSP, structure-based pharmaceutical polymorph design and organic molecular materials discovery.

</details>


### [76] [Non-equilibrium cavity pumping of electronic molecular polaritons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35116)
*Yassir El Moutaoukal,Rosario R. Riso,Henrik Koch*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该工作研究单分子与多光子腔模强耦合的“光子集体”区域，发展约束QED Hartree-Fock方法，实现固定平均光子数的相干态场，并以光子密度调控分子性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统腔光-物质强耦合通常依赖大量分子集体耦合到同一模式；本文关注其光子对应情形，即单个分子与泵浦腔稳态中的大量光子作用。由于Pauli-Fierz哈密顿量的本征态不携带横向电场，该状态不能简单视为光-物质系统的激发态。

Method: 在强耦合QED Hartree-Fock波函数中用Lagrange乘子约束光子数，并对所有参数自洽最小化能量。所得约束参考态对应具有平均光子数的相干态，腔模有效自由场频率被乘子降低。

Result: 应用于苯和苯-水复合物时，泵浦腔使分子极化，并像静态经典场一样改变分子间相互作用；对过氧化氢，该场可重塑扭转势和远红外扭转谱。

Conclusion: 量子化平均场描述可自然导出经典驱动场极限，光子密度因此成为腔改性化学的一个控制参量。

Abstract: Strong light-matter coupling in optical cavities offers a non-invasive route to modify molecular properties, and it is usually reached by collectively coupling many molecules to the same mode. Here we consider the photonic counterpart of the collective regime, in which a single molecule interacts with a cavity mode holding many photons, a steady state of the pumped cavity. Since the eigenstates of the Pauli-Fierz Hamiltonian carry no transverse electric field, such a state cannot be described as an excited state of the light-matter system. We therefore constrain the photon number of the strong coupling QED Hartree-Fock wave function with a Lagrange multiplier and self consistently minimize the energy with respect to all parameters. The constrained reference carries the field of a coherent state with an average number of photons, generated by a cavity mode whose effective free-field frequency is lowered by the multiplier. We apply this framework to benzene and a benzene-water complex, where the pumped cavity polarizes the molecules and modifies their interaction as with a static classical field. For hydrogen peroxide, the field can reshape the torsional potential and the far-infrared torsional spectrum. The classical driven field limit thus emerges from a quantized mean field description, and the photon density becomes a control parameter for cavity-modified chemistry.

</details>


### [77] [Experimental and numerical study of shock wave propagation in water generated by pulsed arc electrohydraulic discharges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35305)
*Wen Chen,Olivier Maurel,Christian La Borderie,Thierry Reess,Antoine Silvestre de Ferron,Mohammed Matallah,Gilles Pijaudier-Cabot,Jacques Antoine,Franck Rey-Bethbeder*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 采用EUROPLEXUS有限元程序模拟水中由脉冲电弧电液放电爆炸产生的冲击波传播，并将注入电能简化为局部水焓增，预测压力与实验一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作为致密气藏替代压裂技术研究的一部分，探索利用动态爆破产生的冲击波提高岩石渗透率，诱导岩石微裂纹而非局部裂缝。

Method: 将脉冲电弧电液放电注入电能简化为水中局部焓增，使用EUROPLEXUS有限元代码进行流体快速动力学计算，模拟水中爆炸冲击波传播及压力。

Result: 预测压力与实验结果一致；模拟还给出了冲击波传播特性，可作为水下结构设计和水中爆炸工程参考。

Conclusion: 该简化模拟方法能有效预测水中爆炸冲击波压力，并为相关工程应用提供依据。

Abstract: The objective of this study is to simulate the propagation of the shock wave in water due to an explosion. The study is part of a global research program on the development of an alternative stimulation technique to conventional hydraulic fracturing in tight gas reservoirs aimed at inducing a distributed state of microcracking of rocks instead of localized fracture. We consider the possibility of increasing the permeability of rocks with dynamic blasts. The blast is a shock wave generated in water by pulsed arc electrohydraulic discharges. The amplitude of these shock waves is prescribed by the electrohydraulic discharges which generate high pressures of several kilobars within microseconds. A simplified method has been used to simulate the injected electrical energy as augmentation of enthalpy in water locally. The finite element code EUROPLEXUS is used to perform fluid fast dynamic computation. The predicted pressure is consistent with the experimental results. In addition, shock wave propagation characteristics predicted with simulation can be valuable reference for design of underwater structural elements and engineering of underwater explosion.

</details>

### [78] [Uncertainty Quantification for the Fission Matrix Method: A Rigorous Mathematical Framework and Computationally Efficient Alternatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33161)
*Valerio Mascolino*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对裂变矩阵法(FM)中蒙特卡洛统计不确定性向keff和裂变源传播通常不够严格的问题，提出全协方差Delta法，并在JSI TRIGA Mark-II的OpenMC模型上与对角协方差近似及系数重采样比较，最终建议实际默认采用对角Delta法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有FM不确定度传播常假设蒙特卡洛系数相互独立，但未根据实际协方差检验这一假设；因此需要严格传播FM系数不确定性，并评估更廉价替代方法的可靠性。

Method: 推导全协方差Delta方法，将FM系数的协方差传播至keff和裂变源；对比对角协方差Delta近似与直接重采样FM系数；采用OpenMC构建JSI TRIGA Mark-II反应堆模型，覆盖all-rods-out情形及FM-CRd控制棒插值与组合策略，并以100次独立蒙特卡洛运行作为经验基准。

Result: all-rods-out下，三种估计与经验基准在keff上相差小于5.7%、裂变源相差小于1%；对角近似偏差的符号在两个统计量间翻转。采用全局固定源FM生成策略时，对角、重采样和独立运行估计彼此约在2%内，但比全协方差估计低约10%，归因于协方差采样噪声。大非对角协方差近似对称，传播时大多抵消，而非此前假设的小或负值。FM-CRd下keff与精确扰动参考在60 pcm内，约两个标准差，裂变源小于0.7%；对角和重采样估计接近，且保守约两倍。

Conclusion: 考虑到全协方差内存代价过高，推荐将对角Delta法作为实际默认方法，而全协方差仅用于对系统进行一次性的不确定性表征。

Abstract: The fission matrix (FM) method recasts neutron transport in matrix form, enabling fast, interpolation-based reactor calculations from a pre-computed database of Monte Carlo-derived coefficients. Propagation of the underlying Monte Carlo statistical uncertainty to the FM eigenvalue and eigenvector is rarely rigorous: prior approaches assume independent coefficients without testing this against their actual covariance. This work derives a full-covariance Delta-method framework for propagating uncertainty to keff and the fission source, and evaluates it with two cheaper alternatives, a diagonal-covariance approximation and direct resampling of the FM coefficients, using an OpenMC model of the JSI TRIGA Mark-II reactor. For the all-rods-out case, these three estimators and an empirical benchmark of 100 independent Monte Carlo runs agree within 5.7% for keff and 1% for the fission source. The sign of the diagonal approximation's bias flips between the two statistics levels, so it must be checked case by case. With a global fixed-source FM generation strategy, the diagonal, resampling, and independent-run estimators agree within about 2% and lie roughly 10% below the full-covariance estimate, a gap attributed to covariance sampling noise. The large off-diagonal covariance is nearly symmetric and mostly cancels when propagated, rather than being small or negative as previously assumed. For the FM control-rod interpolation and combination strategy (FM-CRd), keff matches an exact perturbed reference within 60 pcm (about two standard deviations) and the fission source within 0.7%, while the diagonal and resampling estimators agree closely and are conservative by about a factor of two. Given this and the prohibitive memory cost of the full covariance, the diagonal Delta method is recommended as the practical default, with the full covariance reserved for one-time characterization of a system.

</details>


### [79] [When does the moment basis matter in lattice Boltzmann methods?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33328)
*Alessandro De Rosis*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 论文研究中心矩LBM中正交化矩基是否改变碰撞方案：这由速度集几何决定。作者给出两基等价条件，并证明在D2Q9/D3Q15上剪切率不受正交化影响，而在D3Q19/D3Q27上若四阶矩不以剪切率松弛则方案改变；矩形格子还会改变输运系数。静止平衡内积正交基通常更稳健、更准确，D3Q27计算成本最多增加2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中心矩LBM中常通过正交化矩基改善稳定性与精度，但正交化是否会改变碰撞模型、何时保持相同流体力学与输运系数尚不清晰。需要从碰撞算子的共轭结构出发，明确正交化影响的条件及不同格子的差异。

Method: 将碰撞写成共轭形式 T^{-1}ΛT；设两矩基由常数矩阵A联系，推导等价条件 [Λ,A]_{ij}=A_{ij}(λ_i−λ_j)=0，并指出物理态上仅非守恒矩对应列有影响。对速度集为 {0,±1}^d、对称权重的格子，证明正交化到剪切矩只能通过面/体对角线传播；分析D2Q9、D3Q15、D3Q19、D3Q27及矩形格子。比较静止平衡内积正交基等方案，并通过Taylor-Green DNS评估稳定性、精度和计算成本。

Result: D2Q9与D3Q15中剪切率不受正交化影响；D3Q19与D3Q27中，只要四阶矩不以剪切率松弛，正交化就会改变方案。矩形格子上独立体速率导致正交对应方案不再共享已发表方案的输运系数；已发表方案本身在静止平衡内积下正交，仅差一个一致性所需松弛项。当方案不同但共享流体力学时，静止平衡内积正交基在最不稳定流向的稳健性不差于其他方案超过几个百分点，常更稳健，且对Taylor-Green DNS更准确；经非正交变换和共轭松弛矩阵计算，D3Q27成本最多增加2%。

Conclusion: 正交化并非普遍改变中心矩LBM，其影响取决于速度集几何和四阶矩等非守恒矩的松弛率。选择在静止平衡内积下正交的基通常可保证静止线性稳定性，并提升或不显著损害稳健性与精度，计算开销很小。

Abstract: Whether orthogonalising the moment basis changes a central-moment lattice Boltzmann scheme is decided by the geometry of the velocity set. The basis enters the collision only through the conjugate $\mathit{T}^{-1} \Lambda \mathit{T}$, so two bases related by a constant matrix $\mathit{A}$ define exactly the same scheme if and only if $[\Lambda,\mathit{A}]_{ij}=\mathit{A}_{ij}(\lambda_i-\lambda_j)$ vanishes, and on physical states only its columns for the non-conserved moments matter. We prove that on lattices with velocities in $\{0,\pm1\}^d$ and symmetric weights orthogonalisation reaches the shear moments only through the face and body diagonals: whatever the orthogonalisation, the shear rate is free on D2Q9 and D3Q15, whereas on D3Q19 and D3Q27 the scheme changes whenever the fourth-order moments do not relax at the shear rate. On rectangular lattices, where the symmetry is broken, an independent bulk rate yields an orthogonal counterpart that does not share the transport coefficients of the published scheme, which is itself orthogonal in the inner product of the rest equilibrium up to the one relaxation entry that consistency requires. Where the formulations differ but share their hydrodynamics, a basis orthogonal in the inner product of the rest equilibrium, which guarantees linear stability at rest, was never less robust in the least stable flow direction by more than a few per cent, often much more robust, and the more accurate against a Taylor-Green DNS. Computed through the non-orthogonal transforms with a conjugated relaxation matrix, it costs at most $2\%$ more on D3Q27.

</details>


### [80] [Stochastic Physics-Constrained Operator Inference for Complex Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33380)
*Changhong Mou*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出 SPOpInf：一种非侵入式随机物理约束算子推断方法，从数据学习二次-线性漂移和扩散幅值，并用 EM 联合校准确定性与随机部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加性噪声驱动的二次非线性系统广泛存在于科学与工程中，也常作为湍流降阶模型；现有 OpInf 仅用最小二乘拟合确定性漂移，难以同时校准噪声、保持能量守恒二次结构并处理结构不可辨识性。

Method: SPOpInf 最大化 Euler-Maruyama 增量似然，将残差协方差视为未知量；在优化内部施加线性等式约束，精确强制能量守恒二次结构；采用上三角参数化消除全二次张量的结构不可辨识性；通过 EM 迭代交替更新漂移系数（加权最小二乘）和噪声水平（残差方差更新）。

Result: 在五个具有间歇性和混沌的随机基准系统中，SPOpInf 比 OpInf 更准确恢复确定性算子，约束满足到机器精度，准确估计噪声幅值并复现长期统计；在随机 Burgers 方程和准地转双涡流中，得到长期稳定的非侵入随机 ROM，而无约束替代方法会发散。

Conclusion: SPOpInf 能联合学习稳定且物理一致的随机降阶模型，为二次非线性随机动力系统提供有效的非侵入式数据驱动建模方法。

Abstract: Quadratically nonlinear systems driven by additive noise arise throughout science and engineering, both as stochastic dynamical systems in their own right and as reduced-order models (ROMs) of turbulent flows. We propose stochastic physics-constrained operator inference (SPOpInf), a non-intrusive method that learns a stochastic differential equation, i.e., a quadratic--linear drift and a diffusion amplitude, from data. Unlike operator inference (OpInf), which fits a deterministic drift by least squares, SPOpInf maximizes an Euler--Maruyama increment likelihood in which the residual covariance is itself an unknown, so the deterministic and stochastic components are calibrated jointly; it enforces the energy-preserving quadratic structure exactly, through linear equality constraints imposed inside the optimization; and it works in an upper-triangular parametrization that removes the structural non-identifiability of the full quadratic tensor. The resulting optimization is solved by an expectation--maximization (EM)-based iterative learning algorithm that updates the drift coefficients and the noise levels in turn: each iteration requires only an inexpensive weighted least-squares solve for the drift and a residual-variance update for the noise. With five stochastic benchmark systems that show intermittency and chaos, SPOpInf recovers the deterministic operators more accurately than OpInf, satisfies the imposed constraints to machine precision, accurately estimates the noise amplitudes, and reproduces long-time statistics. Applied to a stochastic Burgers equation and a quasi-geostrophic double-gyre flow, the same estimator yields non-intrusive stochastic ROMs that remain stable over long times where unconstrained alternatives blow up.

</details>

### [81] [Pre-registered tests of solid-state-physics-inspired LLM compression: a cluster-level negative result at small-language-model scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34292)
*Jun-qiang Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 用三个月自主研究代理流程检验五种固态物理启发的压缩映射在预训练语言模型上的有效性；在预注册与3σ门控下，多数假设被否证，注意力衰减更支持拉伸指数/临界或玻璃态而非Kohn近视绝缘体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验面积律/Kohn近视性一粒子密度矩阵衰减能否迁移到Transformer注意力与表示压缩，并以严格预注册和否定性结果发布避免事后拟合。

Method: 将假设分为距离面（P001 Wannier、P002 tight-binding）与秩面（P003 DMRG截断MLP、P005 Wilson-RG、P011 tensor-train嵌入）；在GPT-2-medium、Pythia-160M及至7–8B模型上，以git预注册、3σ PASS/SHELVE门、聚类框架和只追加目录评估注意力-距离拟合、稀疏性、TT bond dimension与surprisal相关性、困惑度代价等。

Result: P005在Phase 1被抢先；Phase-3四个试点中三个被否证。GPT-2-medium中16个中层层头有12个的注意力-距离更符合拉伸指数；tight-binding截断使困惑度+96%；Pythia-160M的Wannier稀疏度0.054±0.004与PCA、random-Haar、identity基线无差别。秩面上TT bond dimension不追踪surprisal（r=0.016 vs 预注册0.65）且格式膨胀而非压缩；P003混合：缩放主张被搁置（r=-0.434），MPO前提在stage-0失败，跨论文检查从r=0.523崩至0.047，两个跨论文检查均为零结果。

Conclusion: 结果反转了“多数注意力头像Kohn近视绝缘体”的预注册预测，指向临界、玻璃态或重尾机制；该反转限于测试的≤350M规模，而秩面无收益结论在7–8B仍成立。贡献包括预注册+3σ+聚类框架+只追加目录的方法纪律、四个预注册负结果与完整数据发布、以及该反转；目录含18项已结研究，其中17项为负。

Abstract: We report a three-month autonomous research-agent program testing five solid-state-physics-inspired compression mappings on pretrained language models, with predictions committed to git before any pilot data and a 3-sigma gate deciding PASS or SHELVE. The common anchor -- area-law / Kohn-nearsighted decay of the one-particle density matrix -- has a distance face (P001 Wannier, P002 tight-binding) and a rank face (P003 DMRG-truncated MLPs, P005 Wilson-RG, P011 tensor-train embeddings). P005 was pre-empted at Phase 1; three of four Phase-3 pilots were falsified. On the attention face, GPT-2-medium attention-versus-distance is best fit by a stretched exponential in 12 of 16 median-layer heads once probe padding is excluded, and a tight-binding cutoff costs +96% perplexity (P002); on Pythia-160M the Wannier sparsity 0.054 +/- 0.004 is indistinguishable from PCA, random-Haar and identity baselines (P001). On the rank face, per-token tensor-train bond dimension does not track surprisal (r = 0.016 vs a pre-registered 0.65) and the format inflates rather than compresses (P011). P003 is mixed: its scaling claim shelved (r = -0.434), its MPO premise died at stage-0, and its cross-paper check, r = 0.523 as first written, collapses to 0.047 under the same correction, leaving both cross-paper checks null. The results invert the pre-registered prediction that most attention heads behave like Kohn-nearsighted insulators, pointing instead to critical, glassy or heavy-tailed regimes; the inversion is specific to the <= 350M scale tested, while the rank-face no-gain result held to 7-8B. We contribute the pre-registration + 3-sigma + cluster-framing + append-only-catalogue discipline -- including why our own enforcement gate was designed but not deployed -- four pre-registered negative results with full data release, and the inversion. The catalogue holds eighteen concluded studies, seventeen negative.

</details>


### [82] [R-transforms for non-Hermitian block random matrices: a spherical integral approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31730)
*Pierre Bousseyroux*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 将非厄米随机矩阵的球面积分与 R-变换推广到固定 K×K 分块随机矩阵，构造秩-K 球面积分、H-变换和矩阵值 R-变换，并用复本方法推测独立分块矩阵和的从属关系，以确定谱边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有球形积分/R-变换方法主要处理无固定分块结构的非厄米随机矩阵；需要刻画具有固定 K×K 块结构的矩阵和及其联合谱行为，进而确定大矩阵谱边界。

Method: 引入秩-K 球面积分，在 2K×2K 重叠矩阵上定义标量 H-变换，并定义编码联合分块结构的矩阵值 R-变换；使用复本方法推导独立分块矩阵和的经验谱从属关系。

Result: 得到独立分块矩阵和的一个猜想性从属关系，并建立了包含固定块结构的非厄米随机矩阵 R-变换框架。

Conclusion: 该框架为确定具固定分块结构的大非厄米随机矩阵谱边界提供方法，将 spherical-integral/R-transform 理论推广到分块情形；但核心从属关系仍为猜想。

Abstract: We extend the spherical-integral approach to $\mathcal{R}$-transforms of non-Hermitian random matrices to random matrices with a fixed $K \times K$ block structure. We introduce a rank-$K$ spherical integral defining a scalar $H$-transform on $2K \times 2K$ overlap matrices and an associated matrix-valued $\mathcal{R}$-transform encoding the joint block structure. Using the replica method, we derive a conjectural subordination relation for sums of independent block matrices. This framework provides a method for determining the spectral boundaries of large non-Hermitian random matrices with a fixed block structure.

</details>


### [83] [Connecting finite-size scaling and renormalization-group flows in Anderson localization on random graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31872)
*Ignacio García-Mata,Weitao Chen,John Martin,Bertrand Georgeot,Gabriel Lemarié*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文研究小世界随机图上安德森相变，比较本征函数矩的有限尺寸标度与多分形维数 D_q 的重整化群流，发现单参数标度成立，且 D_1 的 RG 轨迹并不必然意味着双参数标度或 Kosterlitz-Thouless 型临界行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清小世界随机图上安德森相变中，信息维 D_1 的 RG 流所暗示的双参数标度与 KT 型临界行为是否真实，并建立有限尺寸标度与多分形 RG 描述之间的联系。

Method: 分析本征函数矩的有限尺寸标度；计算多分形维数 D_q 的 RG 流，特别是 q<1/2 的情形；构造刻画本征函数矩流的 beta 函数，并检验不同无序强度下能否塌缩为单一函数。

Result: D_q 的 RG 流重现了此前 D_1 的定性结构；q<1/2 的结果证实强无序局域相具有强多分形性，并表现出多分形行为与局域行为的清晰分离。同时，本征函数矩的 beta 函数在不同无序强度下塌缩为单一函数，明确确认了单参数标度性质。

Conclusion: D_1 所呈现的一族 RG 轨迹不必意味着双参数标度和 KT 型临界行为；要刻画这些性质，应考虑不同观测量，例如大 q>1/2 与小 q<1/2 的本征函数矩，它们对应不同的临界行为，尤其对应不同的临界指数。

Abstract: We investigate the relation between two complementary descriptions of the Anderson transition on small-world random graphs: finite-size scaling of eigenfunction moments and renormalization-group (RG) flows of multifractal dimensions $D_q$. The RG flows recover the qualitative structure previously reported for the information dimension $D_1$, while their extension to other $D_q$ with smaller moment orders $q<1/2$ confirms the strongly multifractal nature of the localized phase characterized by a clear separation between multifractal and localized behaviors at strong disorder. On the other hand, we show that we can construct another beta function characterizing the flow of eigenfunction moments, whose collapse onto a single function across different disorder strengths provides a clear confirmation of single-parameter scaling property. These results indicate that the family of RG trajectories observed for the information dimension $D_1$ need not imply two-parameter scaling and Kosterlitz-Thouless like critical behavior. To characterize these properties, one should consider different observables, such as large $q>1/2$ and small $q<1/2$ eigenfunction moments, which are associated with distinct critical behaviors, in particular different critical exponents.

</details>


### [84] [Dense auto-hetero associative memories applied to noisy communication channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32605)
*Elena Agliari,Andrea Alessandrelli,Adriano Barra,Alberto Fachechi*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该论文将相互作用的模块化Hebbian网络从成对交互扩展到高阶稠密交互，实现了可扩展的模式解缠，并给出统计力学相图、模拟验证及通信/解码应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前模式解缠仅在成对交互的模块化Hebbian网络中成立，可处理的模式数量受限；需要突破容量瓶颈，使网络在存储更多模式的同时仍能自发分离混合模式。

Method: 引入稠密模块化网络：模块内自联想耦合与模块间异联想耦合均推广为高阶Hebbian相互作用；选取合适的交互阶数，并基于Guerra插值进行统计力学分析，推导序参量自洽方程、绘制解缠可行区域的相图，并用Monte Carlo模拟验证。

Result: 在合适交互阶数下，网络能解缠混合模式，且存储模式数随模块大小线性增长，而成对版本在该区间失效；相图给出解缠区域，模拟与理论一致；解缠可作为解码原语，用于从Hebbian张量和无标签混合流中重建隐藏模式，并支持一种将消息token作为隐藏模式掩码混合传输、由网络动力学解码的概念验证通信协议。

Conclusion: 高阶稠密模块化Hebbian网络为模式解缠提供了可扩展的吸引子解码机制；在强信道损坏下能优雅退化，而传统安全传输流程会突然失效，因此适用于鲁棒解码、模式重建和通信等任务。

Abstract: Networks of interacting Hebbian networks have recently been shown to perform a task beyond associative memory, namely \emph{pattern disentanglement}: when fed with a spurious mixture of stored patterns, the different modules spontaneously specialize on, and retrieve, the different constituents of the mixture. So far, this capability has only been established for pairwise interactions, which limits the number of patterns that can be handled. Here we introduce a dense extension of these modular networks, in which both the auto-associative couplings within each module and the hetero-associative couplings among modules are promoted to higher-order Hebbian interactions. We show that, with a suitable choice of the interaction orders, the network disentangles mixtures while storing a number of patterns that scales linearly with the module size, a regime where its pairwise counterpart fails. Through a statistical-mechanical analysis based on Guerra's interpolation, we derive the self-consistency equations for the order parameters and draw the phase diagrams identifying the region where disentanglement is achieved; these predictions are confirmed by Monte Carlo simulations. Finally, we show that disentanglement provides a natural decoding primitive, and we illustrate it with two applications: the explicit reconstruction of all the hidden patterns from the Hebbian tensors and a stream of unlabeled mixtures, and a proof-of-concept communication protocol in which each message token is transmitted as a masked mixture of hidden patterns and decoded by the network dynamics. Owing to its attractor-based decoding, the protocol degrades gracefully under strong channel corruption, where conventional secure-transmission pipelines fail abruptly.

</details>


### [85] [Trimers, pairs and localisation on disordered random graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32768)
*Alexei Vazquez*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 论文研究高阶网络中的群体交互能否由图上的成对动力学涌现，并以 Anderson 局域化为判据考察其大规模稳定性；发现稀疏随机图上可出现三体束缚态 trimer，且其对无序比单粒子/二体更脆弱约一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶网络通常把群体交互当作给定条件，本文追问：若群体交互由低阶成对动力学生成，它何时在大尺度上仍然重要并稳定存在。

Method: 将 k 体束缚态视为涌现超边，其哈密顿量项位于图的顶点和边上；用 Borromean 条件刻画无 (k-1) 体面束缚的 k 体束缚态，并通过热力学极限下 k 重符合探测。在 Bethe 格上求阈值，区分 expander 图具有而格点缺失的两类 sector，并在无序图上用 cavity/Anderson 转变分析局域化。

Result: 稀疏随机图上，三个可区分粒子在在位吸引 U 和跃迁 t 下先形成 trimer 而非任意 pair 束缚。Bethe 格给出 pair 阈值闭式解及两类 sector。无序图上涌现超边在稀有深势位点成核并先于其组成部分局域化：紧凑 trimer 带宽窄、对位点能量有三倍敏感性，Anderson 转变在无序 1.6t，而单粒子为 17t、pair 为 4t；trimer 的 Fermi liquid 在 1.5t 到 1.7t 之间线以上变为 glass，而同密度单粒子 liquid 需 15t 到 16t。该转变可理解为具有复合体跃迁和有效无序的单粒子 cavity 转变。

Conclusion: 由成对动力学生成的群体交互对异质性的脆弱性比生成它的网络高约一个数量级。

Abstract: Higher-order networks take group interactions as given. I ask what happens when a group interaction is made by pairwise dynamics on a graph, and follow it through the transition that decides whether it matters at large scale, Anderson localisation. An emergent hyperedge is a $k$-body bound state of a Hamiltonian whose terms live on the vertices and edges of the graph, Borromean when none of its $(k-1)$-body faces is bound, detected on a finite graph by a $k$-fold coincidence that survives the thermodynamic limit while the $(k-1)$-fold ones vanish. Sparse random graphs produce one from the simplest pairwise dynamics: three distinguishable particles with on-site attraction $U$ and hopping $t$ bind into a trimer before any pair binds. The thresholds are obtained on the Bethe lattice, in closed form for the pair, in both sectors that an expander graph has and a lattice lacks, the one a condensate sees and the one a Fermi liquid sees. On a disordered graph the emergent hyperedges nucleate on rare deep sites and localise before their parts: the compact trimer, with a narrow band and a threefold sensitivity to site energies, undergoes the Anderson transition at a disorder of $1.6t$, against $17t$ for a single particle and $4t$ for a pair, and the Fermi liquid of trimers is a glass above a line between $1.5t$ and $1.7t$, where a liquid of single particles at the same density needs $15t$ to $16t$. The transition is the single-particle cavity transition with the composite's hopping and effective disorder. A group interaction made by pairwise dynamics is an order of magnitude more fragile to heterogeneity than the network that makes it.

</details>


### [86] [Improved Learning of Molecular Energetics Through an Electron-Wise Joint Charge Density and Energy Objective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32858)
*Vadim Ionas,Jonas Elsborg,Felix Ærtebjerg,Arghya Bhowmik*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 提出一种基于图神经网络的联合学习框架，同时预测电子密度与分子能量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高精度杂化泛函如B3LYP计算分子能量代价高，而GGA电子密度较易获得；希望利用电子密度中的丰富空间信息来提升并加速高精度能量学习。

Method: 采用图神经网络，共享一个等变分子表示，并设置电子密度预测头和分子能量预测头；联合训练模型预测GGA电子密度和B3LYP分子能量。

Result: 电子密度分布中的空间信息可提升并加速精确能量的学习；共享等变表示使模型能在统一框架内学习互补的分子性质。

Conclusion: 该工作为近期电荷密度学习框架在原子尺度模拟中的实际应用提供了一条有前景的新路径。

Abstract: We present a graph neural network-based learning framework trained to jointly predict electron densities and molecular energies. The model is trained to predict electron densities calculated with a Generalized Gradient Approximation (GGA) functional while simultaneously learning to predict molecular energies obtained with hybrid functional calculations at the B3LYP level of theory. Our results indicate that the rich spatial information in electron density distribution can be used to improve and accelerate the learning of accurate energies. By sharing an equivariant molecular representation across density and energy prediction heads, the model learns complementary molecular quantities within a unified framework. This provides a new promising route for practical use cases of many recent charge density learning frameworks for atomic scale simulations.

</details>


### [87] [Localization in tight-binding models with power-law distributed couplings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34767)
*Maximilian Weigmann,Luca Schaefer,Barbara Drossel*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文数值比较了一维/二维紧束缚模型中幂律分布耦合下Laplacian矩阵与邻接矩阵的谱及本征模局域化：邻接矩阵在足够大系统下所有模均局域；Laplacian因守恒律保留少量跨越系统模，并导致参与比分布及其与特征值关系的幂律尾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Laplacian矩阵和邻接矩阵对应不同类型的动力学过程，因此需要理解在幂律无序耦合下，二者谱性质与本征模局域化行为的差异，尤其是守恒律、维度与连通性对扩展模和弛豫模的影响。

Method: 对一维和二维紧束缚模型，设定耦合强度服从幂律分布；针对不同系统尺寸和连通度，数值对角化Laplacian矩阵与邻接矩阵；比较本征谱、本征模局域化特性，重点分析参与比及其与特征值的关系，并给出唯象解释。

Result: 邻接矩阵在足够大系统尺寸下所有本征模均局域；Laplacian矩阵由于守恒律，总存在一小部分跨越系统尺度的扩展模，导致参与比概率分布及其与特征值关系呈现幂律尾。一维中这些幂律的指数随耦合分布指数连续变化；二维中当耦合分布指数大于0.75时，具有最大弛豫时间的模由跨越系统转为局域。

Conclusion: Laplacian矩阵与邻接矩阵的局域化行为本质不同：守恒律使Laplacian保持少量扩展模并产生幂律统计；维度、耦合分布指数和连通性共同控制扩展-局域转变及最大弛豫时间模的性质。

Abstract: We study the localization properties of 1D and 2D tight-binding models with power-law distributed couplings by comparing the spectrum and the localization properties of the eigenmodes of the Laplacian and the adjacency matrix, using numerical diagonalization of these matrices for different system sizes and connectivities. These two matrices are relevant for different types of dynamical processes. While all eigenmodes of the adjacency matrix are localized for sufficiently large system sizes, the Laplacian matrix always leads to a small proportion of system-spanning modes due to a conservation law, and therefore to power-law tails in the probability distribution of the participation ratio and its relation to the eigenvalues. In one dimension, the exponent of these power laws change continuously with the exponent that characterizes the distribution of couplings. In two dimensions, the modes with the largest relaxation times change from system-spanning to localized when the exponent of the distribution of couplings becomes larger than 0.75. We provide phenomenological explanations for all these findings.

</details>


### [88] [Exact probability distributions of complex spacing ratios in non-Hermitian random matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35230)
*Kohei Kawabata*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文从精确联合本征值概率密度出发，推导了非厄米高斯随机矩阵系综 AI† 与 AII† 的有限 N 复间距比分布，并给出解析结果与数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复间距比统计可用于诊断开放量子系统中的谱关联与非可积性，但针对非厄米随机矩阵、特别是有限 N 的精确分布仍缺乏系统结果。

Method: 基于精确联合本征值概率密度，分别处理由复对称矩阵实现的 AI† 系综和由复自对偶矩阵实现的 AII† 系综；对 AII† 给出任意 N 的精确代数表达式，对 AI† 在 N=3 时解析得到归一化一维积分表示并进行渐近分析，同时用非厄米随机矩阵直接数值对角化验证。

Result: AII† 中求得任意 N 的精确代数表达式，并显式计算 N=3,4,5,6 的分布与代表性矩；AI† 中得到 N=3 的归一化一维积分表示，发现三次能级排斥具有对数修正，角密度存在非解析贡献；数值对角化确认了解析结果。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了有限 N 下非厄米高斯系综复间距比的精确解析结果，揭示了 AI† 类特有的渐近行为，为利用复间距比研究非厄米与开放量子系统谱统计提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The complex spacing ratio is the complex displacement from a reference eigenvalue to its nearest neighbor divided by the corresponding displacement to its next-to-nearest neighbor. Its statistics provide a useful diagnostic of spectral correlations and nonintegrability in open quantum systems. Here, starting from the exact joint eigenvalue probability densities, we derive finite-$N$ complex-spacing-ratio distributions for the Gaussian ensembles of non-Hermitian random matrices in classes AI$^{\dag}$ and AII$^{\dag}$, realized by complex symmetric and complex self-dual random matrices, respectively. In class AII$^{\dag}$, we obtain an exact algebraic expression for arbitrary $N$ and explicitly evaluate the distributions and representative moments for $N=3, 4, 5, 6$. In class AI$^{\dag}$, although the joint density retains a noncompact integral over nonunitary eigenvector degrees of freedom, we analytically derive a normalized one-dimensional integral representation for $N=3$ and determine the asymptotic behavior, including a logarithmic correction to the cubic level repulsion and a nonanalytic contribution to the angular density. We further confirm these analytical results through direct numerical diagonalization of non-Hermitian random matrices.

</details>


### [89] [Attraction-controlled torque organization and rotational states in frictional granular matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35270)
*Kiwamu Yoshii*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 通过二维摩擦颗粒材料的数值模拟，研究颗粒间吸引力如何影响应力传递、力矩组织与颗粒旋转；吸引力通过重构摩擦接触网络调控宏观流变，并可在特定条件下诱导应力坍缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 吸引力虽为无心力且不直接对颗粒施加力矩，却显著改变接触网络和颗粒旋转，因此需要阐明其在摩擦颗粒材料应力传递与流变行为中的作用机制。

Method: 对二维摩擦颗粒材料进行数值模拟，分析颗粒间吸引力对接触网络、应力、力矩组织和颗粒旋转的影响，并对剪切应力进行吸引与排斥贡献分解。

Result: 吸引力产生低剪切率应力状态，并在干系统剪切增稠附近选择高应力状态，而高剪切率增稠态几乎不变；直接吸引贡献仅占总应力一部分，排斥贡献显著增强；吸引力提高配位数和力矩幅值，并可反转局部力矩符号相关性；低堆积分数与中等剪切率下出现应力坍缩，力矩和旋转被强烈抑制但接触网络仍有限。

Conclusion: 吸引力通过重组摩擦接触网络控制宏观流变，表明接触网络结构是连接吸引相互作用与应力传递、力矩组织及颗粒旋转的关键。

Abstract: In this study, we numerically investigate how interparticle attraction affects stress transmission, torque organization, and particle rotation in a two-dimensional frictional granular material. Although the attraction is a central force and exerts no direct torque on the particles, it strongly modifies the contact network and particle rotation. Attraction produces a low-rate stress state and selects the high-stress state near the shear-thickening regime of the dry system, while leaving the high-rate thickened state almost unchanged. By decomposing the shear stress, we find that the direct attractive contribution accounts for only a fraction of the total stress, while the repulsive contribution is strongly enhanced. Attraction increases the coordination number and torque amplitude and can reverse the local torque-sign correlation. At low packing fractions and intermediate shear rates, a stress-collapse state emerges with strongly suppressed torque and rotation despite a finite contact network. These results show that attraction controls macroscopic rheology through reorganization of the frictional contact network.

</details>
