### [90] [Growth and Interface Engineering of Superconducting TiN on Sapphire by Thermal-Laser Epitaxy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31721)
*Anthony Hyatt,Anand Ithepalli,Eegene Clara Chung,Yorick A. Birkholzer,Brendan Faeth,Huili Grace Xing,David A. Muller,Darrell G. Schlom,Debdeep Jena*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 利用热激光外延（TLE）在蓝宝石上生长TiN，发现提高生长温度可改善输运性能，但高温高氨压会导致TiN/蓝宝石界面空洞；引入850°C TiN种子层后抑制界面退化，并获得迄今蓝宝石上TiN最低的10 K电阻率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TLE可扩展分子束外延的压力、温度和生长速率范围，并支持原位激光退火、高纯缓冲层生长和难熔元素高效蒸发，因此有望用于低损耗超导谐振器和约瑟夫森结异质结。研究旨在探索TiN在蓝宝石上的TLE生长及其界面质量与输运性能。

Method: 采用热激光外延在蓝宝石衬底上生长TiN，并在最高1150°C范围内研究生长温度对输运性能的影响。针对高温和高氨压引起的蓝宝石表面反应及界面空洞，先以850°C生长初始TiN种子层以缓解界面退化。

Result: 生长温度升至1150°C时TiN输运性能改善，但优化TiN所需的高氨压和高温会促进蓝宝石表面反应，在TiN-衬底界面形成空洞，增加界面面积、引入悬挂键，并可能损害隧道势垒异质结。使用种子层后，获得超导转变温度5.8 K、剩余电阻率比12.2、10 K电阻率1.19 μΩ·cm，为目前报道的蓝宝石上TiN最低值。

Conclusion: TLE生长TiN/蓝宝石可通过高温优化输运性能，但必须用低温种子层抑制界面反应和空洞形成。该策略实现了高质量超导TiN，有利于低损耗超导器件和约瑟夫森结异质结的工程化制备。

Abstract: Thermal-laser epitaxy (TLE) extends the accessible pressure, temperature, and growth-rate regimes of conventional molecular-beam epitaxy, enabling in situ laser annealing, high-purity buffer-layer growth, and efficient evaporation of refractory elements. These capabilities make TLE a promising platform for engineering low-loss superconducting resonators and Josephson-junction heterostructures. Here, we study the TLE growth of TiN on sapphire and observe improved transport properties with increasing growth temperature up to 1150 C. Unfortunately, we observe that the high ammonia pressure and elevated substrate temperatures required for optimal TiN properties promote reactions at the sapphire surface, resulting in voids at the TiN-substrate interface. These defects increase interfacial surface area, introduce dangling bonds, and could compromise tunnel-barrier heterostructures. We mitigate this degradation using an initial TiN seed layer grown at 850 C. With the seed layer, we achieve a superconducting transition temperature of 5.8 K, a residual resistivity ratio of 12.2, and a resistivity of 1.19 micro-ohm cm at 10 K, which, to our knowledge, is the lowest reported for TiN grown on sapphire.

</details>


### [91] [Quantum Geometric Origin of Yu-Shiba-Rusinov States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31920)
*Rui-Xing Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作重新审视s波超导体中磁性杂质的Yu-Shiba-Rusinov（YSR）问题，揭示YSR束缚态结构具有量子几何起源。关键量为低能电子Bloch带投影算符的动量空间平均，其矩阵秩、正本征值和本征矢分别决定YSR态的数目、能量和对称表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典YSR理论主要关注电子色散、交换耦合等因素，尚未阐明正常态波函数几何如何影响YSR束缚态。作者旨在建立正常态量子几何与局域YSR谱之间的直接联系。

Method: 提出基于动量空间平均Bloch带投影算符的量子几何理论，分析该矩阵的秩、正本征值及本征矢与YSR束缚态数目、能量和对称表示的对应关系；并将理论应用于单层1H-NbSe2中Nb或Se位点的磁性点杂质。

Result: 发现具有非平庸正常态量子几何的超导体可拥有比平庸情形更多的YSR束缚态，即使二者电子色散相同；在单层1H-NbSe2中，其单一超导能带的几何纹理强制产生三个对称性不同的YSR通道。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了YSR束缚态的量子几何起源，并开辟了通过局域YSR谱探测正常态波函数几何的新途径。

Abstract: In this work, we revisit the classic Yu-Shiba-Rusinov (YSR) problem of magnetic impurities in $s$-wave superconductors and uncover a quantum geometric origin of the YSR bound-state structure. The key geometric quantity is the momentum-space average of Bloch band projector over low-energy electrons, whose matrix rank, positive eigenvalues, and corresponding eigenvectors directly control the number, energies, and symmetry representations of the YSR states, respectively. As a result, a superconductor with nontrivial normal-state quantum geometry can host more bound states than its trivial counterpart, even if they share the same electronic dispersion. We apply our theory to monolayer $1H$-NbSe$_2$ and find that the geometric texture of its single superconducting band enforces three symmetry-distinct YSR channels for a magnetic point impurity at Nb or Se sites. Our work opens a route to probing normal-state wavefunction geometry through local YSR spectroscopy.

</details>


### [92] [Spin pumping reveals vortex-mediated angular-momentum dissipation at the superconducting transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32947)
*Maximilian Mangold,Alex Burg,Franz Weidenhiller,Thomas N.G. Meier,Hiroto Adachi,Lin Chen,Christian Back*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 在 Py/Nb 异质结中，超导临界温度以下一个窄温区内自旋泵浦诱导的磁阻尼显著增强，超过正常态值，并与可移动 Abrikosov 涡旋的涡旋蠕变耗散相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统准粒子图像认为进入超导态后准粒子输运冻结，自旋泵浦效率应降低；本文旨在检验超导态下是否存在额外自旋角动量耗散通道。

Method: 采用铁磁共振/自旋泵浦测量 Py/Nb 异质结的磁阻尼随温度变化，并将其与 Nb 层从有限电阻涡旋蠕变态到钉扎涡旋态的转变进行关联。

Result: 在超导转变温度以下很窄的温度区间内，磁阻尼增强并超过正常态值；该增强与 Nb 层的有限电阻涡旋蠕变区重合，并在冷却进入钉扎涡旋态后消失。

Conclusion: 移动的 Abrikosov 涡旋为自旋角动量耗散提供了额外通道；铁磁共振可作为探测超导体中耗散涡旋动力学的灵敏探针。

Abstract: Spin pumping into superconductors is generally expected to become less efficient below the critical temperature $T_\textrm{c}$ because quasiparticle transport freezes out. Here we report a pronounced enhancement of the spin pumping-induced magnetic damping in Py/Nb heterostructures, confined to a narrow temperature interval immediately below the superconducting transition. The damping exceeds its normal-state value and therefore cannot be explained within the conventional quasiparticle picture of spin transport. The enhancement coincides with the finite-resistance vortex-creep regime of the Nb layers and disappears upon cooling into the pinned vortex state, indicating that mobile Abrikosov vortices provide an additional channel for spin-angular-momentum dissipation. These results establish ferromagnetic resonance as a sensitive probe of dissipative vortex dynamics in superconductors.

</details>


### [93] [Superconducting Pairing Symmetry on Geometric-Algebra Foundations via the Scalar-Projection Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33806)
*Youping Dai*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文用几何代数 Cl_{3,0} 重构超导配对低能理论：把常规 BCS 及 s/p/d 波等配对映射到八维实多向量空间，通过两级投影与 grade-0 几何积恢复标量块和哈密顿量，并给出配对通道二分、Fierz 重排、BdG 量子几何、Fierz 符号对偶及层析重建误差界等结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统复矩阵形式虽物理等价，但配对通道的单重态/三重态二分、Fierz 重排、量子几何结构等缺乏统一的代数根源。作者希望用 Cl_{3,0} 给出透明代数表述，并用于 CsV3Sb5 手性态争议、Weyl 半金属模型和 UTe2 两相分析等具体问题。

Method: 在 Cl_{3,0} 中构造八维实多向量表示：矩阵块表示为 Clifford 多向量，取其 grade-0 投影几何积提取标量块以构建哈密顿量；采用两级投影。利用费米子恒等式 B†(V)=-B†(V^T) 导出配对通道二分，Fierz 重排由映射 Φ:G↦Ge31 实现（e31^2=-1）。建立 kernel/matrix 字典，并进一步研究 BdG 量子几何分解、四维 Minkowski 代数中的 Fierz 符号对偶及线性响应探针下的层析重建。

Result: 该重构在物理上等价于标准复矩阵表述。有效 kernel 层面 grade 0⊕3 对应自旋单重态，grade 1⊕2 对应三重态；配对矩阵层面单重态平面为 span{e2,e31}。BCS 极限具有双重 grade 恒等式：kernel grade 0（标量胶）与顶点 grade 2 双向量（iσ2↔e31）。三项代数结果包括： (i) BdG 量子几何分解定理及 null-texture 判据，用于 CsV3Sb5 手性态争议； (ii) 四维 Minkowski 代数中的 Fierz 符号对偶，并扩展到手性反转的双节点 Weyl 半金属模型； (iii) 线性响应探针模型下层析重建的误差界与条件数结构。示例还给出 glue-generation 机制、一圈代数重整化流（其 feeding constant 大小与 su(2) Casimir 一致）、闭式 μ=0 节点间谱及 UTe2 两相分析。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个与常规超导配对理论物理等价但代数结构更透明的 Cl_{3,0} 框架，将配对对称性、Fierz 重排、BdG 量子几何与若干唯象分析统一起来；其代数判据和符号对偶有望帮助澄清 CsV3Sb5 手性态争议，并推广至 Weyl 半金属及 UTe2 等强关联超导体系。

Abstract: We formulate the low-energy theory of superconducting pairing in the geometric algebra $Cl_{3,0}$. Conventional BCS mean-field theory and its extensions ($s$-, $p$-, $d$-wave, etc.) have algebraic counterparts in an eight-dimensional real multivector space. The construction is a two-level projection: matrix blocks are represented as Clifford multivectors, and their grade-0 projected geometric products extract the scalar blocks from which Hamiltonians are built. The result is an algebraic reconstruction, physically equivalent to the standard complex-matrix formulation. In this reconstruction, the dichotomy of pairing channels acquires an algebraic root: it follows from the fermionic identity $B^\dagger(V)=-B^\dagger(V^{\mathsf T})$, and the Fierz rearrangement is realized by the map $\Phi:G\mapsto Ge_{31}$ (with $e_{31}^2=-1$). This yields a kernel/matrix dictionary: at the effective-kernel level grades $0\oplus3$ correspond to spin singlets and grades $1\oplus2$ to triplets; at the pairing-matrix level the singlet plane is $\mathrm{span}\{e_2,e_{31}\}$. The BCS limit has a double grade identity: kernel grade 0 (scalar glue) and vertex grade 2, a bivector ($i\sigma_2\leftrightarrow e_{31}$). We state three algebraic results: (i) a decomposition theorem for BdG quantum geometry with a null-texture criterion, applied to the chiral-state controversy in CsV$_3$Sb$_5$; (ii) a Fierz sign duality in the four-dimensional Minkowski algebra, with a chirality-reversal extension to two-node Weyl-semimetal models; (iii) an error bound and condition-number structure for tomographic reconstruction under a linear-response probe model. Illustrations include a glue-generation mechanism, a one-loop algebraic renormalization flow whose feeding constant coincides in magnitude with the $\mathfrak{su}(2)$ Casimir, closed-form $\mu=0$ inter-node spectra, and a UTe$_2$ two-phase analysis.

</details>


### [94] [Unconventional superconductivity in locally non-centrosymmetric CeNi$_2$As$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35004)
*Félix Morineau,Jan Knapp,Javier Landaeta,Thomas Lühmann,Lea Richter,Petra Knappova,Soumen Ash,Sushma Lakshmi Ravi Sankar,Konstantin Semeniuk,Elena Hassinger,Christoph Geibel,Manuel Brando,Daniel F. Agterberg,Andrew P. Mackenzie,Seunghyun Khim*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 在CaBe2Ge2型CeNi2As2单晶中发现重费米子超导，为局域非中心对称体系提供新平台，其超导凝聚源于与UBe13定量相似的非相干正常态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受UTe2和CeRh2As2多重超导相及局域反演对称破缺的启发，亟需寻找更多具有局域非中心对称结构的新型重费米子超导材料。

Method: 生长CaBe2Ge2同构的CeNi2As2单晶，并研究其超导性质与正常态行为。

Result: 成功获得超导，可与CeRh2As2对比；超导凝聚来自非相干正常态，且该正常态定量上类似UBe13。

Conclusion: 该发现不仅对局域非中心对称结构中的超导，也对非常规超导本身提出了深刻问题。

Abstract: Recent years have seen intense research on heavy fermion superconductivity, inspired by the discovery of multiple superconducting phases in UTe$_\text{2}$ and CeRh$_\text{2}$As$_\text{2}$. In the latter material, two superconducting phases observed under applied magnetic field oriented along the crystallographic $\textit{c}$ direction are associated with local inversion symmetry breaking. To advance understanding of these phenomena, it is highly desirable to find further examples of heavy fermion superconductivity in materials with locally non-centrosymmetric structure. We have succeeded in this quest, by growing single crystals of the CaBe$_\text{2}$Ge$_\text{2}$ isomorph of CeNi$_\text{2}$As$_\text{2}$. The resulting superconductivity brings more than we had foreseen. In addition to providing the opportunity to compare and contrast with that of CeRh$_\text{2}$As$_\text{2}$, the condensation occurs from an incoherent normal state quantitatively similar to that of UBe$_\text{13}$. Our findings therefore raise profound questions not just about superconductivity in the locally non-centrosymmetric structures, but about unconventional superconductivity itself.

</details>


### [95] [Low-Temperature Stabilization of $δ$-NbN Superconducting Thin Films through Energy-Selective Ion Beam Sputtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35091)
*Yifan Liu,Junwoo Lee,Mingyu Xu,Weiwei Xie,Robert J. Cava,Qi Hua Fan*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作利用室温磁控溅射结合新型能量选择性离子源，独立调控离子能量与通量，实现高质量 NbN 超导薄膜的可控生长，并显著改善其超导转变特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向可扩展量子器件集成与 CMOS 兼容制造，需要在低温甚至室温条件下制备高质量超导薄膜，并克服传统方法在低温稳定高相纯度 NbN 方面的限制。

Method: 采用室温磁控溅射和新型能量选择性离子源生长 NbN 薄膜，通过独立控制离子能量和离子通量来调控表面动力学与结晶路径；随后用超导输运测量、X射线衍射、X射线光电子能谱和透射电子显微镜进行表征。

Result: 与常规反应溅射相比，离子束辅助生长使超导转变宽度大幅收窄，表明薄膜相纯度和电子均匀性得到提升。

Conclusion: 能量选择性离子控制是一种通用的非平衡生长策略，可在低温下稳定亚稳超导相，为薄膜量子材料设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Achieving high-quality superconducting thin films at low temperatures is a central challenge for scalable quantum device integration and Complementary Metal-Oxide-Semiconductor (CMOS)-compatible fabrication. Here, we demonstrate a room-temperature magnetron sputtering approach for synthesizing niobium nitride (NbN) thin films using a novel energy-selective ion source that enables independent control of ion energy and ion flux. This capability provides a powerful route to precisely tailor surface kinetics and crystallization pathways, overcoming longstanding limitations in stabilizing high-quality NbN at reduced temperatures. The resulting films were characterized by superconducting transport measurements, X-ray diffraction, X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy, and transmission electron microscopy. Compared with conventional reactive sputtering, ion beam-assisted growth yields a substantial narrowing of the transition width at the superconducting temperature, indicating improved phase purity and electronic homogeneity. Furthermore, this work introduces energy-selective ion control as a general, non-equilibrium route to stabilize metastable superconducting phases at low temperatures, offering a new design paradigm for thin-film quantum materials.

</details>


### [96] [Improving the critical current density of the $\mathrm{V}_{0.59}\mathrm{Ti}_{0.40}\mathrm{Ce}_{0.01}$ alloy superconductor through successive cold-working and annealing at different temperatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35169)
*Asi Khandelwal,SK. Ramjan,Basudev Padhi,L. S. Sharath Chandra,Archna Sagdeo,Kranti Kumar,Sudip Pal,M. K. Chattopadhyay*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 研究了550°C连续冷加工与退火（SCA）对V0.59Ti0.40Ce0.01合金超导性能及磁通钉扎的影响，并与450°C和650°C SCA进行对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: V-Ti合金超导体的临界电流密度Jc强烈依赖钉扎磁通线的微结构缺陷尺寸与分布，因此需要优化SCA温度以平衡缺陷生成、相演化和回复，从而改善宽磁场下的磁通钉扎。

Method: 对V0.59Ti0.40Ce0.01合金进行550°C SCA处理（包括冷轧至50%厚度及后续退火循环），表征其微结构、超导转变温度、Jc和钉扎力密度，并与先前报道的450°C和650°C SCA结果比较。

Result: 550°C SCA过程中Tc随处理步骤逐渐升高；冷轧后首次退火显著提升整个测量场范围内的Jc，后续SCA循环仅带来边际变化。Jc在较宽磁场范围内对磁场依赖较弱，最终冷加工样品在8.5 T仍具有有限Jc。钉扎力密度分析表明低场由晶界主导钉扎，高场由位错和β-α'界面主导。对比不同温度：650°C低场Jc最高，450°C高场性能最好，550°C场依赖最均衡且相对铸态合金Jc提升最大；高场绝对最高Jc出现在450°C第三次SCA后，但550°C仅首次退火即可获得接近值。

Conclusion: 550°C中间退火温度在缺陷生成、相演化和回复之间提供了有效平衡，从而在宽磁场范围内增强磁通钉扎。

Abstract: The critical current density ($J_c$) of V-Ti alloy superconductors is strongly influenced by the size and distribution of microstructural defects that pin magnetic flux lines. In this work, the effect of successive cold working and annealing (SCA) at 550{\deg}C on the microstructure, superconducting properties, and flux-pinning behaviour of the V$_{0.59}$Ti$_{0.40}$Ce$_{0.01}$ alloy is investigated and compared with the previously reported SCA at 450 and 650{\deg}C. During SCA at 550{\deg}C, the superconducting transition temperature increases gradually with successive processing steps. The first annealing after cold rolling to 50% thickness produces a significant enhancement in $J_c$ over the measured field range, whereas subsequent SCA cycles result in only marginal changes. The $J_c$ remains relatively weakly dependent on magnetic field over wide magnetic-field range, and the final cold-worked sample exhibits a finite $J_c$ up to 8.5 T. Pinning force density analysis shows that grain boundaries dominate flux pinning in low magnetic fields, whereas dislocations and $\beta$-$\alpha'$ interfaces become the dominant pinning centres in higher fields. Comparison of the different SCA temperatures shows that 650{\deg}C provides the highest low-field $J_c$, whereas 450{\deg}C gives the best high-field performance. In contrast, SCA at 550{\deg}C provides most balanced field dependence and the largest enhancement in $J_c$ relative to the corresponding as-cast alloy. Although the highest absolute $J_c$ in the high-field regime is achieved after the third SCA cycle at 450{\deg}C, a comparable value is obtained after only the first annealing at 550{\deg}C. These results demonstrate that the intermediate annealing temperature of 550{\deg}C provides an effective balance between defect generation, phase evolution, and recovery, resulting in enhanced flux pinning over a wide magnetic-field range.

</details>


### [97] [Periodic Trends and a Physics-Based Multistage Search for High-$T_c$ Hydride Superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35582)
*Tianran Chen,Taner Yildirim*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作提出一个可解释的多阶段筛选流程，在完整电-声耦合计算前优先筛选二元氢化物超导体；以谱矩 A1 及 αβρ 描述符结合稳定性与粗粒度 DFPT 计算，从超过10万个结构中筛选并在 NaH6 家族中发现 Pm-3m NaH6 在50 GPa下动力学稳定、Allen-Dynes Tc约230 K。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 富氢材料在高压下可能实现常规声子介导超导，但第一性原理电-声耦合计算成本极高，难以对大量候选结构进行全量筛选。

Method: 在200 GPa下采用受控立方 XmHn 原型，引入基于线宽构建的谱矩 A1 作为电-声微扰强度的中间度量；A1 在谐声子软化时仍有限，可筛选含虚频的相关结构但不证明超导或物理可实现性。对动力学稳定立方体系，A1 与费米能级处 H-1s 态密度分数 α、投影费米面描述符 β、原子数密度 ρ 强相关，并用 A1≈hαβρ 启发式总结。进一步将 αβρ 和焓竞争性纳入三阶段演化搜索：多目标结构筛选、谐声子稳定性过滤、粗粒度 DFPT 电-声耦合计算。

Result: 应用于50和200 GPa下超过100,000个二元氢化物结构，识别出候选并优先考虑 NaH6 结构家族。更高精度计算表明 Pm-3m NaH6 在低至50 GPa时动力学稳定，谐近似 Allen-Dynes 估计 Tc≈230 K。

Conclusion: 该框架将电子描述符与逐步更昂贵的稳定性和电-声耦合计算结合，可用于可扩展的氢化物超导探索，但描述符用于排序而非通用 Tc 预测器。

Abstract: Hydrogen-rich materials under pressure are candidates for conventional phonon-mediated superconductivity, but first-principles electron--phonon-coupling (EPC) calculations are computationally demanding. We develop an interpretable multistage workflow for prioritizing binary hydride superconductors before full EPC calculations. Using controlled cubic $X_m$H$_n$ prototypes at 200~GPa, we introduce the spectral moment $A_1$ as an intermediate measure of electron--phonon perturbation strength. In its linewidth-based construction, $A_1$ remains finite as harmonic phonons soften, allowing related structures with imaginary harmonic modes to be screened; it does not establish superconductivity or physical realizability for unstable structures. For dynamically stable cubic systems, $A_1$ correlates strongly with the H-$1s$ density-of-states fraction at the Fermi level, $\alpha$, a projected Fermi-surface descriptor, $\beta$, and atomic number density, $\rho$. These trends are summarized heuristically by $A_1\approx h\alpha\beta\rho$, where $h$ is framework- and pressure-dependent; the descriptor is used for ranking, not as a universal $T_c$ predictor.
  We incorporate $\alpha\beta\rho$ and enthalpic competitiveness into a three-stage evolutionary search comprising multiobjective structural screening, harmonic-phonon stability filtering, and coarse density-functional perturbation-theory (DFPT) EPC calculations. Application to more than 100,000 binary hydride structures at 50 and 200~GPa identifies candidates and prioritizes NaH$_6$ structural families. Higher-accuracy calculations show that $Pm\bar{3}m$ NaH$_6$ is dynamically stable down to 50~GPa and has a harmonic Allen--Dynes estimate of $T_c\approx230$~K. The framework combines electronic descriptors with progressively more expensive stability and EPC calculations for scalable hydride exploration.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mes-hall'></div>

# cond-mat.mes-hall [[Back]](#toc)

### [98] [Electrically switchable one-dimensional quadrupolar excitons in lateral double heterojunctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32392)
*Ryo Kitaura*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文用有效质量双粒子模型研究单层半导体中 WS2-MoS2-WS2 双异质结的一维电荷转移激子，揭示电场可连续调控其从四极态到偶极态的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相邻两个侧向界面为在单一半导体单层内控制一维电荷转移激子提供了新的空间自由度，因此需要理解界面耦合与电场如何决定激子态、Stark 位移和可调性。

Method: 构建 type-II WS2-MoS2-WS2 双异质结模型，采用含屏蔽库仑相互作用的有效质量两粒子哈密顿量，并进行空间分辨求解与投影测试。

Result: 零电场下左右等价界面耦合使激子分裂为奇偶宇称态，永久偶极为零；垂直电场使低能态由二次 Stark 位移的四极叠加连续转为近似线性位移的单界面偶极激子。对 1.5 nm MoS2 条带，结合能约 104 和 100 meV，双重态分裂 4.6 meV，交叉电场 0.87 V/um；投影测试表明最低双重态主导交叉附近响应，条带宽度主要调节耦合而非结合能。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了连续模型路线，用于实现电可重构的一维四极激子，并表明几何宽度可调控低场 Stark 灵敏度。

Abstract: Two neighboring lateral interfaces provide a spatial degree of freedom for controlling one-dimensional charge-transfer excitons within a single semiconductor monolayer. We investigate a type-II WS2-MoS2-WS2 double heterojunction using an effective-mass two-particle Hamiltonian with a screened Coulomb interaction. For equivalent left and right interfaces at zero electric field,inter-interface coupling produces energetically split even- and odd-parity exciton states, each with zero permanent dipole. An electric field perpendicular to the interfaces continuously converts the lower state from a quadrupolar superposition with a quadratic Stark shift into a predominantly single-interface dipolar exciton with an approximately linear shift. The spatially resolved calculation gives binding energies of approximately 104 and 100 meV, a doublet splitting of 4.6 meV, and a crossover field of 0.87 V/um for a representative 1.5-nm MoS2 strip. Projection tests show that the lowest doublet controls the response near this crossover. Strip width tunes the coupling much more strongly than the binding energy, providing geometric control of the low-field Stark sensitivity. These results establish a continuum-model route to electrically reconfigurable one-dimensional quadrupolar excitons.

</details>


### [99] [Bare-Die Antiferromagnetic Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33460)
*Yu Liu,Zhuoting Han,Zexin Feng,Peixin Qin,Zhiyuan Duan,Yuhao Ye,Zengwei Zhu,Chengyan Zhong,Li Liu,Guojian Zhao,Wenbin Shen,Jingyu Li,Sixu Jiang,Xiaoyang Tan,Xiaoning Wang,Ziang Meng,Chengbao Jiang,Zhiqi Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出一种应变介导反铁磁MnIr/PMN-PT边缘处理器，可在500 K、55 T和1.5 Mrad等极端条件下裸片可靠运行，并实现无需数字预处理的模拟识别与自主导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体器件受量子隧穿和电荷机制限制，难以继续微缩、提速并适应极端环境；深空探索中的极端温度、强磁场和辐射使传统电子器件迅速失效，且难以仅靠屏蔽解决。

Method: 利用输入调制的原位自刷新编码机制，在MnIr/PMN-PT反铁磁边缘处理器上直接从原始模拟信号进行非线性特征提取与分类，构建无需时频变换的全模拟计算架构，并集成为全硬件无人机视觉系统。

Result: 器件在高达500 K、55 T磁场和1.5 Mrad辐射下可靠工作；语音识别准确率99.8%，宇航员视觉目标识别准确率100%；无人机视觉系统实现实时指令执行与自主导航，响应频率达太赫兹级，能耗约0.2 fJ/次操作。

Conclusion: 该工作将反铁磁器件功能从存储和逻辑拓展至物理计算，证明其可作为极端环境中高能效自主智能的有前景材料平台。

Abstract: Semiconductor electronic devices are increasingly constrained by fundamental quantum tunneling effects and charge-based mechanisms, which severely limit further miniaturization, write-speed scaling, and environmental robustness of silicon-based technologies. These limitations are particularly prohibitive for deep-space exploration, where extreme temperatures, ultra-strong magnetic fields, and intense radiation rapidly incapacitate conventional electronics without massive shielding. Here, we present an intrinsically resilient, strain-mediated antiferromagnetic MnIr/PMN-PT edge processor that operates reliably as a bare die under temperatures up to 500 K, magnetic fields of 55 T, and radiation doses of 1.5 Mrad. By exploiting an input-modulated in situ self-refreshing encoding mechanism, the device performs nonlinear feature extraction and classification directly from raw analog signals, enabling an analog computing architecture that requires no time-frequency transformation. This architecture achieves 99.8% accuracy in speech recognition without digital preprocessing and 100% accuracy in astronaut visual object recognition. Furthermore, an all-hardware integrated drone vision system demonstrates real-time in situ command execution and autonomous navigation, delivering a terahertz-level response frequency and an ultra-low energy consumption of approximately 0.2 fJ per operation. This work expands the functional scope of antiferromagnetic devices beyond memory and logic, establishing them as a promising materials platform for energy-efficient physical computing and autonomous intelligence in extreme environments.

</details>


### [100] [Nanodiamond Sensing of Stray Fields during Domain Reversal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31950)
*Kin On Ho,Hamza Abudayyeh,Andrew Perez,Hui-Ping Chang,Liang Juan Chang,Junwei Tong,Can Cui,Jean Anne C. Incorvia,Xiaoqin Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 利用承载NV色心系综的纳米金刚石作为局域量子传感器，定量探测CoFeB条带磁畴反转过程中的纳米尺度杂散磁场，并解释边缘、条带外侧和中心三种不同响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁畴反转过程中的纳米尺度杂散磁场测量对理解和优化磁存储与逻辑器件很重要，但现有探针难以同时实现高空间分辨率和低侵入性。

Method: 将纳米金刚石中的NV色心系综作为局部量子传感器，放置在CoFeB条带不同位置，在磁场驱动反转过程中测量ODMR谱；采用全约束拟合流程提取磁场，并用微磁模拟解释静磁边界场。

Result: 观察到三种局域响应：边缘处正频跳变，条带外侧出现意外的负频跳变，中心附近几乎无变化；这些对比信号与微磁模拟给出的静磁边界场定量一致。全约束拟合可在共振部分分辨时仍稳健提取磁场。

Conclusion: NV-ND磁强计是一种简单、可迁移且定量的方法，可用于表征薄膜和自旋电子器件中空间非均匀的磁场分布。

Abstract: Quantitative measurement of nanoscale stray magnetic fields during domain reversal is important for understanding and optimizing magnetic memory and logic devices. Yet, achieving high spatial resolution with minimally invasive probes remains challenging. Here we demonstrate nanodiamonds (NDs) hosting an ensemble nitrogen vacancy (NV) centers as local quantum sensors of domain-reversal stray fields in a CoFeB strip. During magnetic-field-driven reversal, the ND sensors resolve three distinct local responses depending on their positions relative to the strip: a positive frequency jump at the edge, an unexpected negative jump just outside the strip, and a negligible change near the center. These contrasting signals are quantitatively explained by magnetostatic boundary fields from micromagnetic simulations. We further implement a fully constrained fitting procedure for optically detected magnetic resonance spectra, enabling robust field extraction even when resonances are partially resolved. Together, these results establish NV-ND magnetometry as a simple, transferable, and quantitative approach for characterizing spatially heterogeneous magnetic fields in thin-films and spintronic devices.

</details>


### [101] [Magnetic Q-balls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32059)
*A.J. Balseyro Sebastian,Keisuke Ohashi,Muneto Nitta*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究了准一维手性磁体中带电孤子分支，比较反铁磁二阶动力学与铁磁Berry相位动力学对polar Q-ball、Q-kink及带电液滴存在性和性质的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在静态手性能量相同的情况下，底层磁动力学如何决定带电孤子的形成机制与可观测特征仍不清晰，因此需要系统比较不同动力学完成方式。

Method: 采用含DM相互作用、易轴各向异性和Zeeman耦合的准一维手性磁模型，对同一静态磁泛函分别赋予反铁磁二阶时间导数和铁磁Berry相位动力学。在DM选定的螺旋分支上，将问题约化为序参量极角的一维系统，并由约化有效势曲率和非零转折点导出polar Q-ball存在条件；同时分析反铁磁Q-kink的显式剖面、电荷、能量和裂变判据，以及铁磁情形中Berry相位诱导的移位Zeeman场效应。

Result: 反铁磁情形允许的频率窗口是对称的，并可被DM耦合与Zeeman场共同完全关闭；零Zeeman场下支持反铁磁Q-kink，并给出显式解及相关量。铁磁情形中Berry相位使旋转频率表现为移位Zeeman场，北极或南极带电液滴由移位旋转的相反符号选择；铁磁力学问题的形式极点-极点解一般不等价于有限能量磁孤子，因为Berry项不重整化物理哈密顿量。

Conclusion: 带电孤子的具体机制和可实现性依赖于底层磁动力学，即便静态手性能量完全相同，反铁磁与铁磁动力学也会导致不同的孤子分支与物理判据。

Abstract: We study charged soliton branches in quasi-one-dimensional chiral magnetic systems with Dzyaloshinskii--Moriya (DM) interaction, easy-axis anisotropy, and Zeeman coupling. The same static magnetic functional is equipped with two different dynamical completions: an antiferromagnetic model with second-order time derivatives and a ferromagnetic model with Berry-phase dynamics. On the helical branch selected by the static DM interaction, the problem reduces to an analytically tractable one-dimensional system for the polar angle of the order parameter. We derive the existence conditions for polar Q-balls from the curvature of the reduced effective potential and the presence of a nonzero turning point. In the antiferromagnetic case, the allowed frequency window is symmetric and can be completely closed by the combined effect of the DM coupling and the Zeeman field. At zero Zeeman field, the same reduction also supports antiferromagnetic Q-kinks, for which we obtain explicit profiles, charges, energies, and reduced-sector fission criteria. In the ferromagnetic case, the Berry phase makes the rotation frequency act as a shifted Zeeman field. As a result, north- and south-pole charged droplets are selected by opposite signs of the shifted rotation. We also show that a formal pole-to-pole solution of the ferromagnetic mechanical problem does not generally correspond to a finite-energy magnetic soliton, because the Berry term does not renormalize the physical Hamiltonian. These results clarify how charged-soliton mechanisms depend on the underlying magnetic dynamics, even when the static chiral energy is the same.

</details>


### [102] [In-Memory AM Demodulation Using an All-Silicon Independent-Dual-Gate Gain-Cell Memory with $<10^{-22}$ A Leakage Determined by Single-Electron Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32122)
*Katsuhiko Nishiguchi,Toshiaki Hayashi,Kensaku Chida,Takase Shimizu,Gento Yamahata,Seiya Kasai*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出一种全硅独立双栅存储器，利用单电子计数统计推断出低于10^-22 A的漏电流，实现超过1000 s的保持时间，并可用于存内AM解调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向存内传感与计算，超低漏电存储器备受关注；但硅存储器因访问晶体管漏电，难以在模拟信号处理中实现足够长的保持时间。

Method: 制备全硅独立双栅存储器，通过单电子计数统计推断漏电流；利用访问晶体管亚阈值非线性，同时不扰动存储电荷，实现存内幅度调制（AM）解调。

Result: 测得漏电流低于10^-22 A，保持时间超过1000 s；成功演示存内AM解调。

Conclusion: 该存储器为超低功耗信号处理与存内传感提供了CMOS兼容平台。

Abstract: Ultra-low-leakage memories are attracting increasing attention for in-memory sensing and computing. However, achieving sufficiently long retention in silicon memories for analog signal processing remains challenging because of leakage through the access transistor. In this work, we demonstrate an all-silicon independent-dual-gate memory whose leakage current, inferred from single-electron counting statistics, is below $10^{-22}$ A, giving a measured retention time exceeding 1000 s. Owing to the extremely low leakage, the subthreshold nonlinearity of the access transistor can be exploited without disturbing the stored charge, enabling in-memory amplitude-modulation (AM) demodulation. The proposed memory provides a CMOS-compatible platform for ultra-low-power signal processing and in-memory sensing.

</details>


### [103] [Enhancing magnonic frequency combs via geometric nonlinearity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32173)
*Yushun Fang,Weichao Yu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出利用磁化矢量单位模约束导致的几何非线性作为独立机制，通过横向 Floquet 工程产生低阈值磁子频率梳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有磁子频率梳依赖动态非线性，存在高功率阈值和严格动量守恒限制，亟需新的非线性资源。

Method: 基于磁化矢量单位模约束的几何非线性，采用横向 Floquet 工程将横向驱动转化为纵向参量调制；在低频极限下将四粒子过程约化为有效双磁子调制。

Result: 实现了线性区低阈值频率梳产生，调制幅度随驱动场二次方增长，并因几何谐波获得增强的梳齿平坦度。

Conclusion: 该工作深化了对频率梳与磁子非线性相互作用的理解，为提升磁子频率梳性能提供了新的理论基础。

Abstract: Magnonic frequency combs (MFCs) generated via internal magnetic nonlinearities have exhibited rich physics beyond their optical counterparts. However, existing approaches rely on dynamic nonlinearity, which typically demands high power thresholds and stringent momentum conservation. Here we show that the geometric nonlinearity intrinsic to magnetic systems, originating from the unit-norm constraint of the magnetization vector, can serve as an independent nonlinear resource for MFC generation. Through transverse Floquet engineering, this geometric constraint converts a transverse drive into a longitudinal parametric modulation. In the low-frequency limit, the four-particle process reduces to an effective two-magnon modulation, enabling low-threshold comb generation in the linear regime, with the modulation amplitude scaling quadratically with the driving field and enhanced flatness arising from geometric harmonics. These results provide a deeper understanding of frequency combs and magnon nonlinear interactions, and offer a new theoretical foundation for enhancing MFC performance.

</details>


### [104] [Helicity-Controlled Hall Transport in Hybrid Topological Magnetic Textures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32372)
*Anton V. Hlushchenko,Julia Kharlan,Mykhailo I. Bratchenko,Aleksei V. Chechkin,Jarosław W. Kłos,Oleg A. Tretiakov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出统一自旋空间变换框架，将螺旋度作为几何控制参数，调控斯格明子、反斯格明子、双麦纫子及反双麦纫子等混合拓扑磁织构的电流驱动输运，实现霍尔角的连续调节、完全抑制与任意面内方向导向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑磁织构可作为自旋流驱动的信息载体，但其运动通常伴随霍尔效应而发生横向偏转，导致输运效率与可控性受限。

Method: 建立统一的自旋空间变换框架，结合解析理论与微磁模拟，系统研究不同螺旋度的混合自旋织构；通过求解广义Thiele方程分析电流驱动输运与螺旋度的关系。

Result: 解析解表明螺旋度可作为几何控制参数，并给出霍尔角的简洁表达式，使其可连续调谐、完全抑制并沿任意面内方向确定性导向；微磁模拟证实生成的自旋织构在Landau--Lifshitz--Gilbert动力学下保持稳定，并验证了解析预测。

Conclusion: 这些结果确立了螺旋度作为拓扑磁织构可编程输运的通用控制参数。

Abstract: Topological magnetic textures can serve as information carriers driven by spin currents. However, their motion is generally accompanied by a Hall effect that deflects them from the current direction, limiting transport efficiency and controllability. We develop a unified spin-space transformation framework enabling a systematic study of hybrid spin textures with different helicities, such as skyrmions, antiskyrmions, bimerons, and antibimerons. Combining analytical theory with micromagnetic simulations, we establish the relation between helicity and current-driven transport. An analytical solution of the generalized Thiele equation identifies helicity as a geometric control parameter and yields a simple expression for the Hall angle, enabling its continuous tuning, complete suppression, and deterministic steering along arbitrary in-plane directions. Micromagnetic simulations confirm that the generated spin textures remain stable under Landau--Lifshitz--Gilbert dynamics and validate the analytical predictions. These results establish helicity as a versatile control parameter for programmable transport of topological magnetic textures.

</details>


### [105] [Finite-temperature phase transitions across spin symmetries in two-dimensional magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32644)
*Chen Chen,Moritz A. Goerzen,Megha Arya,Lionel Calmels,Yongping Du,Dongzhe Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过大规模蒙特卡洛模拟经典二维海森堡模型，统一研究了从易面、各向同性到易轴区间的有限温度磁性，并揭示BKT区特征温度异常与涡旋-反涡旋对增殖及有限尺寸效应的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本征二维磁体的实验进展要求统一理解不同自旋对称性下的有限温度相变，并澄清磁晶各向异性、自旋对称性和系统尺寸如何决定有限二维磁体中的特征温度尺度。

Method: 采用经典二维海森堡模型和大型蒙特卡洛模拟，连续调节磁晶各向异性以覆盖易面、各向同性和易轴区间；分析磁化强度、热容及BKT物理，并结合理论与数值方法考察涡旋-反涡旋对增殖。

Result: 特征温度受有限尺寸效应与各向异性诱导的自旋对称性共同影响，在各向同性海森堡区间最显著，向易面和易轴极限明显降低。易面区中，由磁化强度和热容独立估计的特征温度显著偏离BKT理论，却与先前XY模型中的异常一致，并与涡旋-反涡旋对增殖相关，表明其加速成核带来的熵增益是该异常的起源。

Conclusion: 有限易面海森堡磁体中的热力学特征温度，包括对BKT理论的偏离，由有限尺寸自旋关联与涡旋增殖共同决定；该结果阐明了有限二维磁体中实验和技术相关温度尺度随磁各向异性、自旋对称性和系统尺寸的演化规律。

Abstract: Recent advances in intrinsic two-dimensional (2D) magnets have created a need for a unified understanding of finite-temperature phase transitions across different spin-symmetry classes. Here, we investigate finite-temperature magnetism in the classical 2D Heisenberg model using large-scale Monte Carlo simulations, continuously spanning the easy-plane, isotropic, and easy-axis regimes. We find that characteristic temperatures are strongly affected by the interplay of finite-size effects and spin symmetry, induced by magnetocrystalline anisotropy, being most pronounced in the isotropic Heisenberg regime and substantially reduced toward the easy-plane and easy-axis limits. Examining Berezinskii--Kosterlitz--Thouless (BKT) physics in the easy-plane regime, we find that the characteristic temperatures independently estimated from the magnetization and heat capacity deviate significantly from those proposed by BKT theory, but match a previously reported anomaly in the XY-model. We report that characteristic temperatures instead are correlated with the proliferation of vortex-antivortex pairs, suggesting the gain in entropy by their accelerated nucleation as the origin of the anomaly. Establishing this connection both theoretically and numerically, we argue that the thermodynamic characteristic temperature in finite easy-plane Heisenberg magnets, including the deviation from BKT theory, is subject to the interplay between finite-size spin correlations and vortex proliferation. These results clarify how experimental and technologically relevant temperature scales evolve with magnetic anisotropy, spin symmetry, and system size in finite 2D magnets.

</details>


### [106] [Topological chiral edge modes in the continuum of a trivial bulk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34000)
*Li Ge*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过给拓扑平庸二维系统引入虚磁通，并在边缘施加非对称耦合，可在体带连续谱内产生手性边缘模（CEMICs）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统手性边缘模通常位于带隙内；本文旨在探究其能否存在于体带连续谱中，并揭示其产生与非厄米机制的关系。

Method: 在二维拓扑平庸系统的边缘施加非对称耦合，等效引入虚磁通；分析能谱、边缘局域性、PT转变以及非厄米趋肤效应。

Result: CEMICs存在于体带连续谱中，其手性由较强耦合决定；边缘局域并非源于非厄米趋肤效应，而是源于非对称耦合结处系统与环境的能量交换所导致的布里渊区PT转变；在宏观极限下，CEMICs的出现与系统尺寸无关，由非对称耦合与其几何平均之间的黄金比例决定。

Conclusion: CEMICs提供了一种区别于非厄米趋肤效应的机制，可在连续谱中实现手性边缘态，并具有尺寸无关的黄金比例判据。

Abstract: Chiral edge modes in topological states of matter are routinely found inside band gaps, which may be deformed and shifted as a function of the lattice momentum. Here we show that they can exist inside a bulk band instead, i.e., in the continuum. These chiral edge modes in the continuum (CEMICs) are induced by introducing an imaginary magnetic flux to an otherwise topologically trivial two-dimensional system, realized by imposing asymmetric couplings along the edge. While the stronger couplings provide a preferred circulation direction, we find that the edge localization of CEMICs does \textit{not} originate from the non-Hermitian skin effect, which is typically associated with systems featuring asymmetric couplings and the open boundary condition. Instead, this edge localization is due to the energy exchange between the system and the environment at the asymmetric coupling junctions, which leads to a parity-time (PT) transition in the Brillouin zone. Intriguingly, the onset of CEMICs is independent of the system size in the macroscopic limit, which is given by the golden ratio between the asymmetric couplings and their geometric average.

</details>


### [107] [(Sub)nanoscale Visualization of Reconstruction-Driven Moiré Exciton Localization and Delocalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34104)
*Medha Dandu,Sriram Sankar,Giovanny Espitia,Rwik Dutta,Patrick J. Hays,Daria Blach,Takashi Taniguchi,Kenji Watanabe,James R. Chelikowsky,Seth Ariel Tongay,Peter Ercius,Jordan A. Hachtel,Mit H. Naik,Archana Raja,Sandhya Susarla*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作利用低温单色电子能量损失谱、第一性原理GW-BSE计算和光学光谱，在实空间揭示WSe2/WS2莫尔超晶格中结构重构程度如何调控莫尔激子的局域与离域，并指出仅凭光学谱中新出现的莫尔激子共振峰不足以判定激子局域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 莫尔激子在扭转双层中的局域机制及其光学特征尚未被实验解析；通常将光谱指纹视为激子局域的标志，但扭转角相关结构重构、实空间局域与光学信号之间的关系仍不清楚。

Method: 采用低温单色电子能量损失谱进行实空间可视化，结合第一性原理GW-Bethe Salpeter方程计算和光学光谱，研究WSe2/WS2莫尔超晶格中随扭转角变化的结构重构程度及其对莫尔激子行为的影响。

Result: 发现结构重构程度和外加应变驱动莫尔激子局域；与主流理解相反，光学谱中出现新的莫尔激子共振并不足以证明激子局域。研究直接可视化了结构重构与激子局域/离域之间的关联。

Conclusion: 重新确立了莫尔激子局域的判据，提出通过调控结构重构和应变来工程化莫尔激子，并为应变感知量子光电器件提供设计原则。

Abstract: Spectral fingerprints in optical absorption and emission have typically been used as a signature of exciton localization in twisted moir\'e bilayers. However, the mechanism by which excitons become confined to specific stacking sites and their associated optical signature is experimentally unresolved. Here, we directly visualize in real space how tuning the change in extent of structural reconstruction leads to localization and delocalization of moir\'e excitons in the WSe2/WS2 moir\'e superlattice. Using cryogenic monochromated electron energy loss spectroscopy, together with first-principles GW-Bethe Salpeter equation calculations and optical spectroscopy, we uncover the physical mechanism that drives the correlation between twist-angle-dependent structural transformations, the real-space localization of moir\'e excitons, and their optical signatures. Surprisingly, and in contrast to the prevailing understanding, we show that the emergence of new moir\'e exciton resonances in the optical spectra alone is insufficient to establish exciton localization. Instead, the extent of structural reconstruction and external strain drives exciton localization, leading to new design principles for engineering moir\'e excitons and strain-aware quantum optoelectronic devices.

</details>


### [108] [Observing Colossal and Tunable Near-field Thermal Radiation with A Highly Sensitive Annular Micro-thermocouple Junction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34355)
*Ken Araki,Wei Han Won,Liping Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过光纤端环形微热电偶结与二氧化硅微球构建球-板近场辐射传热实验平台，在10 nm间隙观测到超黑体极限2600倍（石英）和900倍（掺杂硅）的巨近场热传递，并利用VO2相变实现可调谐增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近场辐射传热可在纳米间隙超过黑体极限，但室温下实现100倍以上增强通常需要小于50 nm的真空间隙；平行板结构受晶圆弯曲和污染物限制，球-板结构虽可测至30 nm，却存在传感器制备复杂、灵敏度低和信号弱等问题。

Method: 在玻璃光纤末端用简单薄膜沉积制备环形微热电偶结，其Seebeck系数为25 μV/K、热阻为8.7×10^6 K/W，并在结下方粘附二氧化硅微球；测量球-板近场辐射热传递，结合涨落电动力学与Derjaguin近似建模，并通过能量传输计算解释机制；进一步利用VO2薄膜发射极相变实现可调谐近场传热。

Result: 在低至10 nm间隙下，石英体系的近场辐射传热超过黑体极限2600倍，掺杂硅体系为900倍；15 nm间隙下，VO2相变可实现最高430倍增强和64%降低的可调谐近场传热；实验数据与理论模型吻合良好。

Conclusion: 该工作为纳米间隙能量传输提供了高精度、可调谐的实验平台，推进了近场辐射传热的实验研究与基础理解。

Abstract: Near-field radiative heat transfer between two objects has been theoretically predicted and experimentally demonstrated to exceed far-field blackbody limit across nanoscale vacuum gap distance enabled by evanescent surface waves coupling, while significant enhancement by more than 100 times usually requires sub-50-nm vacuum gaps around room temperature. This is challenging for parallel-plate configuration with millimeter sample sizes due to intrinsic wafer bow and contaminant particles. Sphere-plate configuration with a microsphere attached to bimaterial cantilevers or micro-thermocouple tips has been used to experimentally demonstrate near-field radiative heat transfer down to 30-nm gaps, but it is much less developed because of the challenges in the sophisticated sensor fabrication, low sensitivity and weak signals. In this work, we overcome these challenges by an annular micro-thermocouple junction fabricated at the end of a glass fiber with straightforward thin-film deposition to achieve high measurement accuracy with large Seebeck coefficient 25 uV/K and thermal resistance 8.7e6 K/W. With a silica microsphere attached underneath the micro-thermocouple junction, we report experimental observation of colossal near-field radiation heat transfer over blackbody limit down to 10-nm gap up to 2600 times with quartz and 900 times with doped silicon. Upon phase transition of VO2 thin film emitter, tunable near-field heat transfer up to 430-fold enhancement is experimentally demonstrated at 15-nm gap with 64% reduction. Experimental data agrees well with rigorous modeling based on fluctuational electrodynamics and Derjaguin approximation, and underlying mechanism is understood by energy transmission calculations. The results will advance the experimental study and fundamental understanding of energy transport at nanoscale gaps.

</details>


### [109] [Probing hydrodynamics in graphene and quark matter via Seebeck coefficient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34544)
*Subhalaxmi Nayak,Jayanta Dey,Sabyasachi Ghosh*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作用弛豫时间近似的Boltzmann输运方程计算Seebeck系数，以探测石墨烯和夸克-胶子等离子体中的集体输运。高载流子密度下结果趋于Mott关系，低密度Dirac流体区域则显著偏离，并与石墨烯实验一致；QGP中也有类似偏离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验Seebeck系数能否作为强关联量子体系中集体流体力学输运的探针，特别是石墨烯的Dirac流体和超相对论夸克-胶子等离子体。

Method: 在流体力学区域采用动理学理论，通过弛豫时间近似求解Boltzmann输运方程获得Seebeck系数；将结果与常规Mott关系比较，并把形式推广到超相对论夸克-胶子等离子体。

Result: 高载流子密度、即费米液体区域，Seebeck系数趋近Mott关系；低载流子密度、即Dirac流体区域，出现对Mott关系的显著偏离，并与石墨烯实验数据吻合。每粒子焓在Dirac流体区的Seebeck系数中起关键作用。推广到QGP后也观察到类似偏离。

Conclusion: Seebeck系数对Mott关系的偏离可作为集体流体力学输运出现的信号，表明石墨烯与夸克物质这两个差异巨大但强关联的量子体系具有相似的流体响应。

Abstract: We investigate the Seebeck coefficient as a probe of collective transport behavior in graphene and quark-gluon plasma using a kinetic-theory approach in the hydrodynamic regime. The Seebeck coefficient is obtained by solving the Boltzmann transport equation in the relaxation-time approximation. At high-charge-carrier density, corresponding to the Fermi-liquid domain, our result approaches the behavior expected from the conventional Mott relation. In contrast, significant deviations from the Mott relation are observed in the low-carrier-density regime, corresponding to the Dirac fluid domain. These facts are in good agreement with experimental Seebeck coefficient data for graphene. This behavior indicates the emergence of collective hydrodynamic transport in graphene. The enthalpy per particle plays a key role for the Seebeck coefficient in the Dirac fluid regime. We extend our formalism to the ultra-relativistic quark-gluon plasma. A similar deviation from the Mott relation is observed, supporting a similar fluid response in the Seebeck coefficient across two markedly different strongly correlated quantum systems - graphene and quark matter.

</details>


### [110] [Sub-20 nm spin textures with arbitrary topological charge stabilized by higher-order interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34706)
*Zhen Sun,Shiwei Zhu,Moritz A. Goerzen,Megha Arya,Changsheng Song,Dongzhe Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作用量子第一性原理参数化的原子自旋模型，预测Janus单层中可形成亚20 nm、|Q|高达5的高拓扑荷磁织构，并证明高阶自旋相互作用是其成核与稳定的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同一体系中不同拓扑荷Q的磁织构对应用极具吸引力。尽管已在中心对称磁体中发现skyrmion与antiskyrmion（|Q|=1）共存，但任意高Q（|Q|>1）织构的实现与稳定机制仍不清楚。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算参数化的原子尺度自旋模型，在Janus单层范德华磁体中模拟高Q自旋织构；研究对象包括skyrmion/antiskyrmion rings和skyrmion bags，并分析其成核机制、热稳定性、坍塌路径，以及高阶自旋相互作用（HOI）和Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用（DMI）的作用。

Result: 预测了亚20 nm、|Q|至5的skyrmion/antiskyrmion rings与skyrmion bags。Rings在零磁场下热稳定；HOI显著提高其能垒而几乎不改变尺寸，并可在无DMI时稳定它们。Skyrmion bags仅在HOI存在时成核，其中四自旋三格点相互作用是防止高Q织构坍塌为铁磁态的关键。此外，还发现先前未报道的宇称依赖坍塌机制。

Conclusion: HOI是一种此前被忽视的高Q拓扑织构成核与稳定机制，为在Janus单层等范德华磁体中设计高拓扑荷自旋织构提供了新思路。

Abstract: Topological magnetization textures play a central role in modern magnetism. For many applications, spin textures with different topological charges $Q$ in the same system are particularly attractive. Although the coexistence of skyrmions and antiskyrmions ($|Q| = 1$) has been reported in inversion-symmetric magnets, extending this to high-$Q$ ($|Q| > 1$) textures with arbitrary charge remains elusive. Here, using an atomistic spin model parameterized from first-principles calculations, we predict the emergence of sub-20 nm high-$Q$ textures in Janus monolayers, van der Waals magnets of growing interest. We explore skyrmion and antiskyrmion rings as well as skyrmion bags with $|Q|$ up to 5, and characterize their nucleation mechanisms, thermal stability, and collapse pathways. We find that higher-order spin interactions (HOI), which extend the conventional bilinear-exchange Hamiltonian, are essential for stabilizing these high-$Q$ spin textures. The rings remain thermally stable at zero magnetic field. HOI substantially enhance their energy barriers while leaving their size nearly unchanged and stabilize them even in the absence of Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction. Skyrmion bags, in contrast, nucleate only in the presence of HOI. In particular, the four-spin three-site interaction is the key ingredient preventing high-$Q$ textures from collapsing into the ferromagnetic state. Finally, we identify previously unreported parity-dependent collapse mechanisms for high-$Q$ textures. Our results establish HOI as an overlooked mechanism for high-$Q$ nucleation.

</details>


### [111] [Moiré Quantum Layer Hall Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34941)
*Jin-Xin Hu,Wen-Bo Dai,X. C. Xie*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作提出利用莫尔工程在偶数层拓扑反铁磁薄膜 MnBi2Te4 中实现量子层霍尔效应（QLHE），并进一步调控出层极化量子反常霍尔（QAH）相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偶数层拓扑反铁磁薄膜中的层霍尔效应（LHE）虽可由垂直位移场调控，但其量子化版本 QLHE 在实验上仍难以实现，因此需要一种可行的实现方案。

Method: 作者理论提出：在 MnBi2Te4 薄膜表面附近放置莫尔控制层，利用其产生的静电超晶格势进行莫尔工程；在保持 PT 对称性的情况下构造体系，并通过弱栅极电场破缺 PT 对称性，或仅在一侧表面引入单个控制层来调控层极化 QAH 相。

Result: 该体系可实现净霍尔电导为零、层霍尔电导量子化为 e^2/h 的 QLHE；弱栅极电场可驱动体系进入层极化 QAH 相；仅单侧控制层时，即使无外加电场也可出现层极化 QAH。微观机制为标量莫尔势诱导出层 U(1) 规范场，并在顶层和底层产生相反的周期性赝磁场。

Conclusion: 该方案为在反铁磁薄膜中实现 QLHE 提供了可行路径，并展示了莫尔工程调控层自由度与拓扑相的有效性。

Abstract: Even-layer topological antiferromagnetic thin films such as MnBi$_2$Te$_4$ exhibit an unconventional layer Hall effect (LHE) arising from a layer-locked Berry curvature. This effect manifests as a layer-polarized anomalous Hall effect that can be controlled by a vertical displacement field. However, achieving a quantized version of the LHE remains experimentally challenging. In this work, we propose that moir\'{e} engineering, using an electrostatic superlattice potential from control layers, can realize the quantum layer Hall effect (QLHE). Specifically, by placing moir\'{e} control layers in close proximity to the surfaces of a MnBi$_2$Te$_4$ thin film without breaking $PT$ symmetry, the resulting system exhibits a vanishing net Hall conductance but a quantized layer Hall conductance of $e^2/h$. A weak gate electric field then breaks $PT$ symmetry and drives the system into a layer-polarized quantum anomalous Hall (QAH) phase. Furthermore, when a single control layer is introduced on only one surface, the layer-polarized QAH emerges even in the absence of an external electric field. We identify the microscopic origin of the QLHE as an emergent layer-$U(1)$ gauge field generated by the scalar moir\'{e} potential, which in turn produces opposite periodic pseudomagnetic fields on the top and bottom layers. Our work provides a feasible pathway toward realizing the QLHE in antiferromagnetic thin films.

</details>


### [112] [Capacitance sensing in bilayer graphene with gate reflectometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35125)
*Tamás Kalmár,Gergő Fülöp,Bálint Szentpéteri,Olivér Kürtössy,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Szabolcs Csonka,Péter Makk*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出一种基于栅极反射法的电容读出方案，用于探测 hBN/WSe2 封装的双层石墨烯异质结，可靠追踪位移场诱导带隙开启，并可用于识别 hBN 与 BLG 的小角度对齐，还提出双谐振器复用读出以提取层极化率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在双层石墨烯拓扑态研究中，现有电容测量多依赖桥式读出或源漏反射测量，作者希望发展一种更可扩展、受样品无序输运通道影响更小的替代读出方法。

Method: 将集总元件 LC 谐振器耦合到异质结顶栅，通过栅极反射法测量 BLG 薄片电容；并与同步 DC 输运测量对比；进一步用固定频率微波读出提取绝缘态与金属态间的电容变化，并用综合电容网络模型解释其来源；最后提出复用的双谐振器读出方案，以同时测量顶栅和底栅电容。

Result: 微波读出能够可靠追踪位移场诱导的带隙开启；相比 DC 输运测量，RF 信号受样品无序导致的渗流输运通道影响更小；还能识别 hBN 与 BLG 之间的小角度对齐，并提取绝缘态到金属态的器件电容变化，电容网络模型可解释该变化。

Conclusion: 栅极反射法是一种高度可扩展且强大的方法，可用于探索范德华异质结中的拓扑相和内部电荷动力学；所提出的双谐振器复用方案有望同时测量顶/底栅电容并提取体系层极化率。

Abstract: In the search for topological states in bilayer graphene, capacitance measurement methods are widely utilized in bridge type readouts or source-drain reflectometry setups. In this paper we demonstrate an alternative readout method based on gate reflectometry. We probe the capacitance of a BLG flake encapsulated between hBN and WSe$_2$ through a lumped-element LC resonator coupled to the top gate electrode of the heterostructure. The microwave readout reliably tracks the opening of a displacement field-induced band gap in the device. Compared to simultaneous DC transport measurements, the RF signal proves to be less affected by the percolating transport channels caused by sample disorder. Furthermore, we use the gate based readout to identify a small-angle alignment between the hBN and BLG flake. From the fixed frequency microwave readout, we extract the device capacitance change between the insulating and metallic state of the bilayer and utilize a comprehensive capacitance network model to explain its origin. Finally, we propose an advanced, multiplexed double-resonator readout scheme capable of simultaneously measuring top and bottom gate capacitances to extract the layer polarizability of the system. This work establishes gate reflectometry as a highly scalable and powerful methodology for exploring topological phases and internal charge dynamics in van der Waals heterostructures.

</details>


### [113] [Plasmon modes in three-dimensional nodal-ring semimetals: tilt and gap effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35373)
*Ipsita Mandal*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 系统研究三维节线半金属中PT对称无隙节线（PTNR）及其有隙对应体系（GNR）的等离激元模式，在低掺杂下用RPA计算响应函数、Drude权重和等离激元色散，并比较无倾斜、面内kx倾斜与轴向kz倾斜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 节线半金属中倾斜和能隙会显著改变费米面与集体激发，尤其部分有隙区域和费米面不完全存活时的等离激元行为缺乏系统理解；需要比较无隙/有隙及不同倾斜方向的影响。

Method: 采用低掺杂线性化哈密顿量，计算无相互作用密度-密度响应函数、Drude权重，并在随机相近似（RPA）下求等离激元色散。分别处理无倾斜、面内kx倾斜和轴向kz倾斜；对部分有隙区域，在小倾斜和小能隙极限推导闭式结果，并针对轴向倾斜中双值局域费米面求态密度与Drude权重闭式。

Result: 面内倾斜GNR中，足够大倾斜或较小化学势使节线部分进入有隙区，仅部分费米面存活；小能隙极限给Drude权重和等离激元频率带来分数幂修正。轴向倾斜GNR的部分有隙窗口呈现双值局域费米面，并获得态密度和Drude权重闭式。进一步分析倾斜与质量隙对等离激元色散及轴向/面内各向异性的调控。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了无隙与有隙情形以及不同倾斜方向之间的系统比较，揭示倾斜、能隙和费米面部分存活对等离激元色散与各向异性的影响，特别指出小能隙导致的分数幂修正等特征。

Abstract: We study plasmon modes in three-dimensional nodal-ring semimetals, considering both the gapless $\mathcal{PT}$-symmetric nodal ring (PTNR) and its gapped counterpart (GNR). Working in the low-doping regime where the linearised Hamiltonian is valid, we compute the non-interacting density-density response function, the Drude weight, and the plasmon dispersion within the random-phase approximation. We treat the untilted configuration as well as in-plane tilt along $k_x$ and axial tilt along $k_z$. For the in-plane tilted GNR, a strong-enough tilt or a small enough chemical potential pushes part of the ring into a partially-gapped regime. Only part of the Fermi surface survives there. We obtain closed-form results for this regime in two limits: a small tilt and a small gap. The small-gap limit produces a fractional-power correction to the Drude weight and the plasmon frequency. For the axially-tilted GNR, the partially-gapped window has a double-valued local Fermi surface for which we obtain the density-of-states and the Drude weights in closed forms. We analyse how the tilt and the mass-gap affect the plasmon dispersion and the anisotropy between axial and in-plane directions. Our results provide a systematic comparison between the gapless and gapped cases and between different tilt directions.

</details>


### [114] [Extracting the anyonic exchange phase from co-tunneling through an open quantum dot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35480)
*Felix Puster,Matthias Thamm,Bernd Rosenow*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析一种由三个量子点接触两两连接三段手征边缘态构成的干涉仪：其中一段可作为开放量子点，实现无需栅压调谐的共振隧穿。通过非平衡Keldysh微扰到隧穿振幅三阶，作者发现干涉电流包含交换两个准粒子的共隧穿贡献，使Laughlin态的交换相位 θ=πν 表现为两种电压配置下Aharonov-Bohm振荡之间的相移，并给出有限温度、有限量子点接触间距下的解析结果及提取θ的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数量子霍尔态的元激发服从任意子统计，其交换相位θ介于玻色子与费米子之间；测量θ是理解任意子统计的核心目标。作者希望用更简单、无需精细栅压调谐的干涉装置实现近期提出的反点干涉仪中的共隧穿机制，从而稳健地提取Laughlin态的交换相位。

Method: 构建三量子点接触两两连接三段手征边缘态的干涉仪，使一段在合适电压下充当开放量子点，频谱连续且隧穿共振无需额外栅压。采用非平衡Keldysh形式计算到隧穿振幅三阶，得到包含双准粒子交换的共隧穿干涉项；推导有限温度和有限量子点接触间距下的闭合解析干涉电流，并比较两种电压配置下的Aharonov-Bohm振荡相移。

Result: 干涉电流中的共隧穿贡献交换两个准粒子，因此对Laughlin态出现交换相位 θ=πν 作为两种电压配置下Aharonov-Bohm振荡之间的相移。若电压引起的包围面积变化被充分屏蔽，θ可从稳健的相位平台差中提取；若同时测量两个漏极的干涉电流，则可避免该屏蔽假设，并可解析量化飞行时间相位，通过受控外推提取θ，系统误差约为百分之几。

Conclusion: 该方案以简单设置实现了反点干涉仪的共隧穿机制，提供了在分数量子霍尔体系中测量任意子交换相位的可行途径，并给出有限温、有限间距下的解析描述及误差可控的相位提取方法。

Abstract: The elementary excitations of fractional quantum Hall states obey anyonic statistics, described by an exchange phase $\theta$ intermediate between the bosonic and fermionic values. We study an interferometer in which three chiral edge segments are pairwise connected by three quantum point contacts, so that, depending on the applied voltages, one of the segments acts as an open quantum dot: its spectrum is continuous, and tunneling through it is resonant without any gate tuning. This setup realizes the co-tunneling mechanism of a recently proposed antidot interferometer in a particularly simple setting. Using a non-equilibrium Keldysh calculation to third order in the tunneling amplitudes, we show that the interference current contains a co-tunneling contribution in which two quasiparticles are exchanged, so that for Laughlin states the exchange phase $\theta=\pi\nu$ appears as the phase shift between the Aharonov-Bohm oscillations measured in two voltage configurations. We obtain a closed analytic expression for the interference current at finite temperature and finite separation between the quantum point contacts and show that, provided voltage-induced changes of the enclosed area are sufficiently screened, the exchange phase can be extracted from a robust phase-plateau difference. Alternatively, simultaneous measurements of the interference currents at two drains avoid this screening assumption and time-of-flight phases can be quantified analytically, allowing the exchange phase to be extracted by a controlled extrapolation with a few-percent systematic error.

</details>


### [115] [Dynamical transition in non-Hermitian Chern insulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35492)
*Zhi-Qiang Zhang,Yuan-Hang Ren,Ming Lu,Hua Jiang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 非厄米Chern绝缘体中波包传播会出现动力学转变：边缘波包的行为不再仅由拓扑陈数决定，而取决于传统拓扑局域与非厄米趋肤效应之间的竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究非厄米拓扑系统中体边对应与波包动力学的关系，特别是传统拓扑边缘态与非厄米趋肤效应共同作用时，边缘初始波包是否仍沿边界传播。

Method: 通过分析非厄米Chern绝缘体中波包的演化，考察边缘初始波包在传统拓扑局域和非厄米趋肤效应竞争下的传播行为，并结合广义布里渊区框架讨论拓扑相变。

Result: 与厄米Chern绝缘体不同，非厄米情形下边缘初始波包可能沿系统边界传播，也可能穿透进入体态；当传统拓扑局域占优时沿边界传播，否则扩散入体。该动力学转变不能仅由陈数决定。

Conclusion: 广义布里渊区框架能可靠预测拓扑相变，但不能单独确定动力学相变；边缘波包传播需考虑非厄米趋肤效应与拓扑局域的竞争。

Abstract: We unveil a peculiar dynamical transition for the propagation of wave packets in non-Hermitian Chern insulators, where the evolution of the topological wave packets at the edge is not solely determined by the topological Chern number. Unlike the Hermitian Chern insulator, where a wave packet initiated at the edge propagates along the system boundary, here it may instead penetrate into the bulk. This behavior is attributed to the competition between the localization induced by conventional topology and the non-Hermitian skin effect. Specifically, when the former dominant, the wave packet will evolve along the boundary; otherwise, it will spread into the bulk. These features demonstrate that while the generalized-Brillouin zone framework reliably predicts topological phase transitions, it does not on its own specify the dynamical transitions.

</details>


### [116] [Superfluid Spin Transport in the Van der Waals Antiferromagnet CrCl$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35513)
*Peisen Yuan,Xiaomin Guo,Vincent Flynn,Benedetta Flebus,Fèlix Casanova,Luis E. Hueso*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在易面范德华反铁磁绝缘体CrCl3中通过非局域器件发现并调控自旋超流输运，其表现为约90微米、弱衰减的超长程自旋输运，并可被强磁场和高温抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋超流有望实现相位梯度驱动、超低损耗的自旋输运，但实验研究受限于缺乏合适材料体系。

Method: 构建非局域器件结构，结合非局域磁子输运测量与理论建模，研究易面范德华反铁磁绝缘体CrCl3。

Result: 在倾斜反铁磁自旋构型下，CrCl3中出现自旋超流，导致约90微米、弱衰减的超长程自旋输运；强磁场和升温会抑制超流态，并恢复非相干磁子的快速指数衰减。

Conclusion: 该发现凸显了二维磁绝缘体中自旋超流的潜力，并确立CrCl3作为下一代自旋电子学中高能效、长距离自旋输运的有前景平台。

Abstract: Over the past decade, the quest for spin superfluidity has moved to the forefront of spintronics, driven by the promise of phase-gradient-driven, ultra-low-loss spin transport. However, experimental investigations remain limited, primarily due to the lack of suitable material systems. Here, we report on the discovery and control of a superfluid spin transport in the easy-plane van der Waals antiferromagnetic (AFM) insulator CrCl$_3$ by a nonlocal device structure. Combining nonlocal magnon transport measurements with theoretical modelling, we demonstrate that spin superfluidity emerges in CrCl$_3$ under canted AFM spin configurations, where it gives rise to ultra-long range (around 90 $\mu$m), weakly decaying spin transport. We also provide direct evidence that strong magnetic fields and elevated temperatures suppress the superfluid state, restoring the rapid exponential decay with distance of incoherent magnons. These findings underscore the potential of spin superfluidity in two-dimensional magnetic insulators and establish CrCl$_3$ as a promising platform for energy-efficient, long-distance spin transport in next-generation spintronic applications.

</details>
